# Go API client for crossly

The seller-facing Crossly API — listings, crossposting, orders, analytics. Authenticate with a Personal Access Token (`Authorization: Bearer crossly_pat_…`) or a seller OAuth token (`crossly_oat_…`), scoped `<domain>:<read|write>`. Buyer endpoints are a SEPARATE API with a separate principal — see the Crossly Buyer API.

## Overview
This API client was generated by the [OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech) project.  By using the [OpenAPI-spec](https://www.openapis.org/) from a remote server, you can easily generate an API client.

- API version: 1.0.0
- Package version: 0.1.0
- Build package: org.openapitools.codegen.languages.GoClientCodegen

## Installation

Install the following dependencies:

```shell
go get github.com/stretchr/testify/assert
go get golang.org/x/oauth2
go get golang.org/x/net/context
```

Put the package under your project folder and add the following in import:

```golang
import crossly "github.com/GIT_USER_ID/GIT_REPO_ID"
```

To use a proxy, set the environment variable `HTTP_PROXY`:

```golang
os.Setenv("HTTP_PROXY", "http://proxy_name:proxy_port")
```

## Configuration of Server URL

Default configuration comes with `Servers` field that contains server objects as defined in the OpenAPI specification.

### Select Server Configuration

For using other server than the one defined on index 0 set context value `sw.ContextServerIndex` of type `int`.

```golang
ctx := context.WithValue(context.Background(), crossly.ContextServerIndex, 1)
```

### Templated Server URL

Templated server URL is formatted using default variables from configuration or from context value `sw.ContextServerVariables` of type `map[string]string`.

```golang
ctx := context.WithValue(context.Background(), crossly.ContextServerVariables, map[string]string{
	"basePath": "v2",
})
```

Note, enum values are always validated and all unused variables are silently ignored.

### URLs Configuration per Operation

Each operation can use different server URL defined using `OperationServers` map in the `Configuration`.
An operation is uniquely identified by `"{classname}Service.{nickname}"` string.
Similar rules for overriding default operation server index and variables applies by using `sw.ContextOperationServerIndices` and `sw.ContextOperationServerVariables` context maps.

```
ctx := context.WithValue(context.Background(), crossly.ContextOperationServerIndices, map[string]int{
	"{classname}Service.{nickname}": 2,
})
ctx = context.WithValue(context.Background(), crossly.ContextOperationServerVariables, map[string]map[string]string{
	"{classname}Service.{nickname}": {
		"port": "8443",
	},
})
```

*InventoryApi* | [**CreateCatalogMatchDismiss**](docs/InventoryApi.md#createcatalogmatchdismiss) | **Post** /v1/catalog-matches/{id}/dismiss | Dismiss a catalog-match suggestion. That product is never suggested for this item again.

*InventoryApi* | [**GetGameLeaderboard**](docs/InventoryApi.md#getgameleaderboard) | **Get** /v1/games/leaderboards | Game leaderboards: for each of Price Guess (wins, then points), scavenger hunts (finished) and speed trades (won), the top ten this week (Monday 00:00 UTC on) under week and of all time under all -- rank, name, named, value and, for Price Guess, score. A name appears only for somebody who chose to be seen by name in Crossly World; everyone else is \&quot;A player\&quot; in their place.
*InventoryApi* | [**GetHome**](docs/InventoryApi.md#gethome) | **Get** /v1/homes/{slug} | A home by its address, as you may see it: open homes to anybody, friends-only homes to mutual follows, locked homes to their owner. 404 when you may not enter.
*InventoryApi* | [**GetHomeMine**](docs/InventoryApi.md#gethomemine) | **Get** /v1/homes/mine | Your Crossly World home: its address, privacy (open / friends / locked), theme, the room, the layout (each piece with what it shows -- things you no longer own are left out at read time) and the guestbook. Made on first read.

*InventoryApi* | [**GetInventoryFacet**](docs/InventoryApi.md#getinventoryfacet) | **Get** /v1/inventory/facets | Distinct brands + categories across this user&#39;s inventory.

*InventoryApi* | [**GetInventorySellThrough**](docs/InventoryApi.md#getinventorysellthrough) | **Get** /v1/inventory/sell-through | Cohort sell-through for a window (default: the 30 days ending 30 days ago) against the previous window of the same length.
*InventoryApi* | [**GetInventorySkuExist**](docs/InventoryApi.md#getinventoryskuexist) | **Get** /v1/inventory/sku-exists | Check whether a SKU is already in use on this user&#39;s inventory.
*InventoryApi* | [**GetInventoryValueHistory**](docs/InventoryApi.md#getinventoryvaluehistory) | **Get** /v1/inventory/value-history | Portfolio value of unsold stock over time, oldest first (one point per nightly snapshot).
*InventoryApi* | [**GetMainStage**](docs/InventoryApi.md#getmainstage) | **Get** /v1/main-stage | The Crossly World Main Stage right now: the live show (title, kind, host lineup), the stream on its big screen while a lineup host is on air, how many people are there (it holds 64 per copy and opens copies as it fills) and what is on next.
*InventoryApi* | [**GetPetMine**](docs/InventoryApi.md#getpetmine) | **Get** /v1/pets/mine | Your Crossly World pets: every kind (cat, dog, slab bot) with whether you own it, when it was earned and the ways to earn it (a quest or a reputation level -- pets are never sold), plus the pet walking beside you and the one greeting visitors at your shop door.
*InventoryApi* | [**GetReputation**](docs/InventoryApi.md#getreputation) | **Get** /v1/reputation/{profile} | Somebody&#39;s public reputation levels by their storefront handle: the level on each track and their nameplate badge. 404 for an unknown handle.
*InventoryApi* | [**GetReputationMine**](docs/InventoryApi.md#getreputationmine) | **Get** /v1/reputation/mine | Your Crossly World reputation: four tracks (trader, collector, seller, host) with points, level, what each level has unlocked and the next unlock, and how the points were earned (recency-weighted; a lost dispute or a failed authentication costs points). Earned from real activity only; nothing buys a level.

*InventoryApi* | [**GetSpatialAuctionRoom**](docs/InventoryApi.md#getspatialauctionroom) | **Get** /v1/spatial/auction/rooms/{sceneId} | What the rostrum says in this room, right now.
*InventoryApi* | [**GetSpatialEmote**](docs/InventoryApi.md#getspatialemote) | **Get** /v1/spatial/emotes | Your Crossly World emotes: every emote pack (unlocked or not, with the quest that unlocks it), your 8-slot emote wheel as stored and as it shows (labels, locks, ready dots), and the moments earned and not yet played.
*InventoryApi* | [**GetSpatialEmployeeConfig**](docs/InventoryApi.md#getspatialemployeeconfig) | **Get** /v1/spatial/employee/config | How the shop employee in one of your rooms is configured.
*InventoryApi* | [**GetSpatialParty**](docs/InventoryApi.md#getspatialparty) | **Get** /v1/spatial/party | Your Crossly World party: party is null when you are in none, else its id, leader and up to 8 members (each with the place and copy they are in right now, or null), plus the invites it has out. invites lists the invites waiting for you.

*InventoryApi* | [**GetWorldRoomFunnel**](docs/InventoryApi.md#getworldroomfunnel) | **Get** /v1/world/rooms/{sceneId}/funnel | Your room&#39;s funnel over the last &#x60;days&#x60; (1-35, default 30): visitors (people, once a day), looks, items opened, checkouts started from the room, paid sales and revenueCents. *InventoryApi* | [**GetWorldShelf**](docs/InventoryApi.md#getworldshelf) | **Get** /v1/world/shelf/{listingSlug} | Where a Crossly listing sits on a shelf in a public room right now (roomSlug, roomName, itemId), or data:null when it is in no room.
*InventoryApi* | [**GetWorldSkusCheapest**](docs/InventoryApi.md#getworldskuscheapest) | **Get** /v1/world/skus/{skuId}/cheapest | The cheapest copy of a catalogue piece for sale on Crossly now, matched by the catalogue id (listingSlug, priceCents, and the room and item when it stands on a shelf), or data:null.

*InventoryApi* | [**ListGameBreakUpcoming**](docs/InventoryApi.md#listgamebreakupcoming) | **Get** /v1/games/breaks/upcoming | Pack breaks staged on shop stages in Crossly World, live first then soonest: the break, its shop, start time, spots (taken or not, buyer masked), hits revealed so far, and buyUrl -- the break&#39;s page on crossly.net, the only place a spot is bought (null once every spot has sold).
*InventoryApi* | [**ListGameHunts**](docs/InventoryApi.md#listgamehunts) | **Get** /v1/games/hunts | Scavenger hunts running now in Crossly World shops and pop-up stalls, ending soonest first: title, shop (name and slug -- for a hunt at a stall, the street slug, with stall.bookingId set), how many tokens, what finishing unlocks (\&quot;10% off\&quot; -- never the code), when it ends and how many people have finished. *InventoryApi* | [**ListGameResultMine**](docs/InventoryApi.md#listgameresultmine) | **Get** /v1/games/results/mine | Your market-game results, newest first: game (price_guess, price_guess_solo, hunt, speed_trade, break_host), the round/hunt/trade/break id, score, whether you won, Bucks prize in cents (0 while prizes are pins only) and detail. meta.unlocks lists the hunt coupons you earned (with their codes); meta.hits the break hits pulled for spots you bought, with the order each ships with.
*InventoryApi* | [**ListHomeMineBuilders**](docs/InventoryApi.md#listhomeminebuilders) | **Get** /v1/homes/mine/builders | Your co-builders: friends you let rearrange your home in the world (move, turn, take out your things, add free furniture; never your bought pieces). Each: userId, name, friends (false once the friendship ended: listed but powerless).
*InventoryApi* | [**ListHomeOfTheWeek**](docs/InventoryApi.md#listhomeoftheweek) | **Get** /v1/homes/of-the-week | Home of the week: open homes by how many different people signed their guestbook in the last 7 days, most first.

*InventoryApi* | [**ListMainStageEvents**](docs/InventoryApi.md#listmainstageevents) | **Get** /v1/main-stage/events | Live and upcoming Main Stage events, soonest first: brand launches, creator meetups, talks, live sales and festival shows, each with its window and host lineup.
*InventoryApi* | [**ListSpatialAuctionRoomLots**](docs/InventoryApi.md#listspatialauctionroomlots) | **Get** /v1/spatial/auction/rooms/{sceneId}/lots | The catalogue: every lot in the session this room is hosting.

*InventoryApi* | [**ListSpatialMoments**](docs/InventoryApi.md#listspatialmoments) | **Get** /v1/spatial/moments | Your moment emotes, newest first: unbox (a Crossly purchase delivered), slab_flip (a card graded through Crossly Grading), handshake (a trade-table shake settled). Each plays once in a room; playedAt is null until then. ready&#x3D;true lists only the unplayed ones.

*InventoryApi* | [**ListSpatialPublic**](docs/InventoryApi.md#listspatialpublic) | **Get** /v1/spatial/public | Public rooms anyone can walk into.

*InventoryApi* | [**ListWorldItemSearch**](docs/InventoryApi.md#listworlditemsearch) | **Get** /v1/world/items/search | Find an item on a shelf anywhere in Crossly World: what public rooms have for sale now, by title or catalogue name (q, 2+ characters). *InventoryApi* | [**ListWorldRoomWants**](docs/InventoryApi.md#listworldroomwants) | **Get** /v1/world/rooms/{sceneId}/wants | Items your room shows but does not sell that visitors said they would buy (\&quot;I&#39;d buy this\&quot;), most wanted first: itemId and how many people.
*InventoryApi* | [**UpdateInventory**](docs/InventoryApi.md#updateinventory) | **Patch** /v1/inventory/{id} | Update an inventory item (partial).
*InventoryApi* | [**UpdateSpatialEmployeeConfig**](docs/InventoryApi.md#updatespatialemployeeconfig) | **Put** /v1/spatial/employee/config | Create or replace the shop employee in one of your rooms.
*InventoryApi* | [**UpdateSpatialShopStaffWage**](docs/InventoryApi.md#updatespatialshopstaffwage) | **Put** /v1/spatial/shop/staff/{grantId}/wage | Set a staff member&#39;s weekly wage.

*ListingsApi* | [**CreateListingBulkCrosspost**](docs/ListingsApi.md#createlistingbulkcrosspost) | **Post** /v1/listings/bulk-crosspost | Bulk crosspost (no delist phase)
*ListingsApi* | [**CreateListingBulkDelete**](docs/ListingsApi.md#createlistingbulkdelete) | **Post** /v1/listings/bulk-delete | Bulk archive + delist
*ListingsApi* | [**CreateListingBulkDelist**](docs/ListingsApi.md#createlistingbulkdelist) | **Post** /v1/listings/bulk-delist | Bulk delist from platforms
*ListingsApi* | [**CreateListingBulkDelistPreview**](docs/ListingsApi.md#createlistingbulkdelistpreview) | **Post** /v1/listings/bulk-delist-preview | Preview which marketplaces a delist would touch
*ListingsApi* | [**CreateListingBulkHardDelete**](docs/ListingsApi.md#createlistingbulkharddelete) | **Post** /v1/listings/bulk-hard-delete | Permanently delete listings (live ones need allowLive)

*ListingsApi* | [**CreateListingBulkUpdate**](docs/ListingsApi.md#createlistingbulkupdate) | **Post** /v1/listings/bulk-update | Bulk update listing fields
*ListingsApi* | [**CreateListingById**](docs/ListingsApi.md#createlistingbyid) | **Post** /v1/listings/by-ids | Fetch hydrated listings by ID
*ListingsApi* | [**CreateListingCheckDuplicate**](docs/ListingsApi.md#createlistingcheckduplicate) | **Post** /v1/listings/check-duplicates | Check whether the seller already owns something matching this title/photo, and what to do about it.
*ListingsApi* | [**CreateListingCombine**](docs/ListingsApi.md#createlistingcombine) | **Post** /v1/listings/combine | Combine duplicate listings into one: sums their stock, delists and archives the rest.
*ListingsApi* | [**CreateListingDiscrepancyResolve**](docs/ListingsApi.md#createlistingdiscrepancyresolve) | **Post** /v1/listings/{id}/discrepancies/{discrepancyId}/resolve | Resolve a detected marketplace-drift discrepancy: accept the platform value, push ours back, relist to apply it, or dismiss.
*ListingsApi* | [**CreateListingImportByUrl**](docs/ListingsApi.md#createlistingimportbyurl) | **Post** /v1/listings/{id}/import-by-url | Attach a real platform listing to this listing by pasting its live URL.
*ListingsApi* | [**CreateListingMagicFill**](docs/ListingsApi.md#createlistingmagicfill) | **Post** /v1/listings/{id}/magic-fill | Auto-fill empty fields on one platform tab from the master listing + AI/deterministic taxonomy resolution.
*ListingsApi* | [**CreateListingMerge**](docs/ListingsApi.md#createlistingmerge) | **Post** /v1/listings/merge | Merge the same item listed on different marketplaces into one listing; all stay live.
*ListingsApi* | [**CreateLiveStreamPinLaunch**](docs/ListingsApi.md#createlivestreampinlaunch) | **Post** /v1/live-streams/{id}/pin-launch | Pin one of your live NFT launches on your live show; viewers mint it from the stream. Returns &#x60;{ ok }&#x60;.
*ListingsApi* | [**CreatePassport**](docs/ListingsApi.md#createpassport) | **Post** /v1/passports | Register a product passport for a Crossly listing you sell, optionally bound to an NFC tag UID (hex). Returns the URL to write to the NFC tag or print as a QR; ownership then follows the item’s paid sales.
*ListingsApi* | [**CreatePassportRevoke**](docs/ListingsApi.md#createpassportrevoke) | **Post** /v1/passports/{id}/revoke | Revoke a passport (a lost or misprinted tag). Refused once the item has sold — the passport is then part of its history.
*ListingsApi* | [**CreatePassportTag**](docs/ListingsApi.md#createpassporttag) | **Post** /v1/passports/{id}/tag | Bind (or re-bind) a passport to the NFC tag you wrote its nfcUrl to: the tag UID (hex), and whether you locked the tag read-only. Refused once the item has sold, for a locked tag, or a tag already on another item.
*ListingsApi* | [**CreateVariationGroup**](docs/ListingsApi.md#createvariationgroup) | **Post** /v1/variation-groups | Group listings you already have: a variation (one product, an axis such as Size) or a purchase-rule group (\&quot;buy any 3\&quot;).
*ListingsApi* | [**CreateVariationGroupGenerate**](docs/ListingsApi.md#createvariationgroupgenerate) | **Post** /v1/variation-groups/generate | Turn one listing into a variation group: each new value becomes its own listing (a copy, with its own price, quantity and SKU) and all of them one group.
*ListingsApi* | [**CreateVariationGroupPublish**](docs/ListingsApi.md#createvariationgrouppublish) | **Post** /v1/variation-groups/{id}/publish | Publish a variation group per platform: one listing with a variation picker where the platform supports it (eBay, Shopify, Etsy), one per option elsewhere. Each platform reports its own outcome.
*ListingsApi* | [**DeleteListing**](docs/ListingsApi.md#deletelisting) | **Delete** /v1/listings/{id} | Delist a listing (optionally narrowed to specific platforms via ?platforms&#x3D;).
*ListingsApi* | [**DeleteLiveStreamPinLaunch**](docs/ListingsApi.md#deletelivestreampinlaunch) | **Delete** /v1/live-streams/{id}/pin-launch | Unpin the launch from your live show. Returns &#x60;{ ok }&#x60;.
*ListingsApi* | [**DeleteRentalOffer**](docs/ListingsApi.md#deleterentaloffer) | **Delete** /v1/rentals/offers/{platformListingId} | Stop offering a listing for rent (existing bookings stand).
*ListingsApi* | [**DeleteVariationGroup**](docs/ListingsApi.md#deletevariationgroup) | **Delete** /v1/variation-groups/{id} | Dissolve a group. Its options stay as ordinary listings.
*ListingsApi* | [**GetListing**](docs/ListingsApi.md#getlisting) | **Get** /v1/listings/{id} | Get one listing with its platform rows.
*ListingsApi* | [**GetListingFacet**](docs/ListingsApi.md#getlistingfacet) | **Get** /v1/listings/facets | Distinct brands + categories across listings + inventory.
*ListingsApi* | [**GetListingSkuExist**](docs/ListingsApi.md#getlistingskuexist) | **Get** /v1/listings/sku-exists | Check whether a SKU is already used by one of this user&#39;s items.
*ListingsApi* | [**GetPricingSetting**](docs/ListingsApi.md#getpricingsetting) | **Get** /v1/pricing/settings | Selling options: own-price match (Crossly sells your items at your own lower price for the same item on another marketplace; dollar-priced copies only) and layaway (buyers may put items on layaway). Both on by default.
*ListingsApi* | [**GetPricingSoldComp**](docs/ListingsApi.md#getpricingsoldcomp) | **Get** /v1/pricing/sold-comps | What one catalog product (market_sku_id, in one condition) actually sold for across Crossly in the last 180 days: median, p25/p75, p10/p90 (null under 10 sales), sale count, last sold, and days from listing to sale (median, p25, p75). Pass marketSkuId (+ optional condition) or one of your listingIds. comps is null until there are 3+ sales from 2+ sellers; holdCode says why there is nothing (no_market_sku, mixed_inventory, below_privacy_floor) — the same reasons a sold_median reprice rule holds.
*ListingsApi* | [**ListListingAiDetections**](docs/ListingsApi.md#listlistingaidetections) | **Get** /v1/listings/{id}/ai-detections | Crossly&#39;s AI-image detections for a listing&#39;s photos.
*ListingsApi* | [**ListListingDiscrepancies**](docs/ListingsApi.md#listlistingdiscrepancies) | **Get** /v1/listings/{id}/discrepancies | List detected marketplace-drift discrepancies for a listing.
*ListingsApi* | [**ListListingIds**](docs/ListingsApi.md#listlistingids) | **Get** /v1/listings/ids | Filter listings → return matching id list (no pagination).
*ListingsApi* | [**ListListings**](docs/ListingsApi.md#listlistings) | **Get** /v1/listings | List active platform listings.
*ListingsApi* | [**ListPassports**](docs/ListingsApi.md#listpassports) | **Get** /v1/passports | Product passports you registered (optionally for one Crossly listing): the code and URL each tag carries.
*ListingsApi* | [**ListRentalOffers**](docs/ListingsApi.md#listrentaloffers) | **Get** /v1/rentals/offers | What you offer for rent.
*ListingsApi* | [**UpdateHotspot**](docs/ListingsApi.md#updatehotspot) | **Put** /v1/hotspots | Tag your listings at points on a story or lookbook photo (shop the look). Replaces that photo’s tags; at most 12.
*ListingsApi* | [**UpdateListing**](docs/ListingsApi.md#updatelisting) | **Patch** /v1/listings/{id} | Edit a listing and fan out update jobs to existing platform listings.
*ListingsApi* | [**UpdateListingCustom**](docs/ListingsApi.md#updatelistingcustom) | **Put** /v1/listings/{id}/customs | Set a listing’s customs: HS code (6–10 digits), country of origin, and countries this item must not ship to on Crossly. Omitted fields are left alone; null (or [] for restrictedCountries) clears.
*ListingsApi* | [**UpdateListingPlatformListing**](docs/ListingsApi.md#updatelistingplatformlisting) | **Patch** /v1/listings/{id}/platform-listings/{plId} | Set one marketplace copy&#39;s price (e.g. the ebay.com copy of a listing also live on ebay.co.uk), in that copy&#39;s own currency.
*ListingsApi* | [**UpdateLiveStreamLineup**](docs/ListingsApi.md#updatelivestreamlineup) | **Put** /v1/live-streams/{id}/lineup | Set a live show’s lineup: your Crossly listings, in the order you will sell them. Buyers see it before they join; a sale of a lineup item while the show is live is recorded at its replay moment.
*ListingsApi* | [**UpdatePricingSetting**](docs/ListingsApi.md#updatepricingsetting) | **Put** /v1/pricing/settings | Turn own-price match and/or layaway on or off.
*ListingsApi* | [**UpdateRentalOffer**](docs/ListingsApi.md#updaterentaloffer) | **Put** /v1/rentals/offers/{platformListingId} | Offer a Crossly listing for rent: day rate, optional week rate, deposit, min/max days, turnaround buffer, and rent-only (off sale while offered). Needs a ship-from address.
*ListingsApi* | [**UpdateVariationGroup**](docs/ListingsApi.md#updatevariationgroup) | **Patch** /v1/variation-groups/{id} | Retitle a group, rename its axis, or change a group&#39;s purchase rule (written into every member&#39;s description).

*MobileApi* | [**DeleteMobilePushToken**](docs/MobileApi.md#deletemobilepushtoken) | **Delete** /v1/mobile/push-tokens | Clear ALL registered push tokens for this user.
*MobileApi* | [**ListMobilePushTokens**](docs/MobileApi.md#listmobilepushtokens) | **Get** /v1/mobile/push-tokens | List registered Expo push tokens (masked).

*NFTMarketApi* | [**CreateNftMarketListingConfirmSale**](docs/NFTMarketApi.md#createnftmarketlistingconfirmsale) | **Post** /v1/nft-market/listings/{id}/confirm-sale | Ops: record a finalized on-Crossly NFT sale (verifies the royalty landed, funds the pot). Admin PAT only.
*NFTMarketApi* | [**GetNftMarketListing**](docs/NFTMarketApi.md#getnftmarketlisting) | **Get** /v1/nft-market/listings | Browse open on-platform NFT listings (Crossly cosmetics for resale in CBX/SOL).
*NeighbourhoodsApi* | [**GetNeighbourhood**](docs/NeighbourhoodsApi.md#getneighbourhood) | **Get** /v1/neighbourhoods/{slug} | One neighbourhood street by slug.

*OrdersApi* | [**CreateLiveShipmentShip**](docs/OrdersApi.md#createliveshipmentship) | **Post** /v1/live-shipments/{id}/ship | Ship a show box with your own label: every order is marked shipped with this tracking and the buyer’s extra postage is refunded.

*OrdersApi* | [**CreateOrderBulkPackingSlip**](docs/OrdersApi.md#createorderbulkpackingslip) | **Post** /v1/orders/bulk-packing-slips | Bulk packing slips PDF.

*OrdersApi* | [**CreateOrderDispute**](docs/OrdersApi.md#createorderdispute) | **Post** /v1/orders/{id}/dispute | Flag an order as disputed.
*OrdersApi* | [**CreateOrderImport**](docs/OrdersApi.md#createorderimport) | **Post** /v1/orders/import | Manually (re-)pull one or more connected platforms&#39; order history for a day-window.

*OrdersApi* | [**CreateOrderMessage**](docs/OrdersApi.md#createordermessage) | **Post** /v1/orders/{id}/message | Message the order&#39;s buyer on the marketplace.
*OrdersApi* | [**CreateOrderPickup**](docs/OrdersApi.md#createorderpickup) | **Post** /v1/orders/pickups | Book a carrier pickup for labelled orders (one carrier per pickup; window at least an hour, up to 14 days ahead).
*OrdersApi* | [**CreateOrderPickupLabel**](docs/OrdersApi.md#createorderpickuplabel) | **Post** /v1/orders/{id}/pickup-label | Buy the carrier label for a Crossly order the buyer collects from a pickup point (debits your shipping wallet). 402 when the wallet is short.

*OrdersApi* | [**CreateOrderRate**](docs/OrdersApi.md#createorderrate) | **Post** /v1/orders/{id}/rates | EasyPost rate quote for an order.

*OrdersApi* | [**CreateOrderTracking**](docs/OrdersApi.md#createordertracking) | **Post** /v1/orders/{id}/tracking | Submit tracking number + carrier and notify the platform.

*OrdersApi* | [**CreateRentalApprove**](docs/OrdersApi.md#createrentalapprove) | **Post** /v1/rentals/{id}/approve | Approve a requested rental (offers with requireApproval): the renter’s held card is charged.

*OrdersApi* | [**CreateRentalClaim**](docs/OrdersApi.md#createrentalclaim) | **Post** /v1/rentals/{id}/claim | Claim part or all of the deposit (cents, a note, photo URLs): within 3 days of the return, or 14 days after the end if it never came back.

*OrdersApi* | [**CreateRentalReturned**](docs/OrdersApi.md#createrentalreturned) | **Post** /v1/rentals/{id}/returned | Mark a rental back with you. You then have 3 days to claim damage before the deposit goes back.
*OrdersApi* | [**CreateRentalShipped**](docs/OrdersApi.md#createrentalshipped) | **Post** /v1/rentals/{id}/shipped | Mark a booked rental shipped, with at least 2 photos of its condition as it leaves (URLs you uploaded) — the evidence any later claim is judged against.
*OrdersApi* | [**GetOrder**](docs/OrdersApi.md#getorder) | **Get** /v1/orders/{id} | Get one order.

*OrdersApi* | [**GetOrderCount**](docs/OrdersApi.md#getordercount) | **Get** /v1/orders/counts | Tab badge counts by status.
*OrdersApi* | [**GetOrderEvidence**](docs/OrdersApi.md#getorderevidence) | **Get** /v1/orders/{id}/evidence | Everything recorded about how this order was packed and how it arrived.

*OrdersApi* | [**GetOrderScanForm**](docs/OrdersApi.md#getorderscanform) | **Get** /v1/orders/scan-forms/{id} | One SCAN form; a queued form is re-read from the carrier and its PDF appears once built.
*OrdersApi* | [**GetOrderShipment**](docs/OrdersApi.md#getordershipment) | **Get** /v1/orders/{id}/shipments | List the parcels an order shipped in.
*OrdersApi* | [**GetOrderWhatnotPackingSlip**](docs/OrdersApi.md#getorderwhatnotpackingslip) | **Get** /v1/orders/{id}/whatnot/packing-slip | A fresh link to Whatnot&#39;s packing slip PDF.
*OrdersApi* | [**GetOrderWhatnotShipment**](docs/OrdersApi.md#getorderwhatnotshipment) | **Get** /v1/orders/{id}/whatnot/shipment | Whatnot shipment for an order (package, label, tracking). Records a label found on it.
*OrdersApi* | [**ListLayaways**](docs/OrdersApi.md#listlayaways) | **Get** /v1/layaways | Your items on layaway (active and paid off). Do not ship an active one: its order moves to needs_fulfillment when paid in full.
*OrdersApi* | [**ListLiveShipments**](docs/OrdersApi.md#listliveshipments) | **Get** /v1/live-shipments | Your show boxes: every buyer’s orders from one live show, to ship together.

*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketAuctionLot**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketauctionlot) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/auctions/lots | Consign a stored, authenticated unit to the next weekly or premier event, with an optional opening price and reserve. *VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketAuctionLotBid**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketauctionlotbid) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/auctions/lots/{id}/bids | Leave or raise your maximum on a lot; the system bids for you up to it. A bid in the last 2 minutes extends the lot by 2 minutes. The winner&#39;s saved card is charged at the close through the order book. ID needed above $10,000.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketAuctionLotWithdraw**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketauctionlotwithdraw) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/auctions/lots/{id}/withdraw | Withdraw your lot before any bid; the unit goes back on your shelf. Returns &#x60;{ ok }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketBlock**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketblock) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/blocks | Place a block buy for an entity: one order booked to the first sub-account in &#x60;plan&#x60; (it pays from its custodian account), split across every sub-account in the plan at the average price, fees included, once it is done and the custodian confirmed each payment. Each share&#39;s cash moves between the entity&#39;s own custodian accounts; its units move only after that settles. Every sub-account must be one you may trade, and its share must fit its limits.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketBlockAllocate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketblockallocate) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/blocks/{orderId}/allocate | Allocate a done block now (the keeper does it too). Returns &#x60;{ outcome, block }&#x60;: allocated, waiting (a payment or transfer is not settled yet), nothing_filled or none.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketBullionFamilyPeg**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketbullionfamilypeg) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/bullion/family-pegs | Set a family-wide spot-pegged quote: one \&quot;any 1 oz gold coin\&quot; quote resting on each product book of a bullion family at the chosen condition (DS, VNDS or Used). When any leg fills it comes off the others and goes back on only for what is left; two fills at once are settled one at a time against what is left. A bid pays from your custodian account. Replaces your live family quote on the same family, condition and side. ID required.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketBullionPeg**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketbullionpeg) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/bullion/pegs | Set a spot-pegged maker quote on a bullion book key: \&quot;spot plus or minus N cents\&quot; or \&quot;plus or minus N basis points\&quot;, with an optional floor and cap. It rests as a pro limit order re-priced every spot tick and is pulled while spot is stale. Replaces your live quote on the same key and side. ID required.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketCollectionWorthWatch**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketcollectionworthwatch) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/collection-worth/watch | Watch every collection item that sits on a vault book key (up to the watchlist cap). Returns &#x60;{ watched, skipped }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketCreatorAttribution**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketcreatorattribution) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/creators/attribution | Use a creator code: the creator earns a capped share of the fee Crossly keeps on your Vault Markets trades for a limited window (paid by Crossly, never by you). Only for people new to Vault Markets, once, and never your own code.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketCreatorCohostInviteRespond**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketcreatorcohostinviterespond) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/creators/cohost-invites/{id}/respond | For creators: accept or decline an invite to co-host a Market Open launch show (&#x60;accept&#x60; true or false). Accepting needs your vault room verified and adds you to the show as a co-host; your room and the Commons then say you co-host it, with the creator-programme disclosure when it applies.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketCreditRepay**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketcreditrepay) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/credit/{id}/repay | Repay from your custodian account, interest first. Omit &#x60;amountCents&#x60; to repay everything; pass &#x60;requestId&#x60; to make a retry safe. Repaid in full, the items are free.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketDcmLink**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketdcmlink) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/dcm/link | Link a partner exchange account to your account (or to a firm sub-account you manage, &#x60;subaccountId&#x60;) so Crossly can show its positions: &#x60;accountRef&#x60; (the account id there) and &#x60;code&#x60; (the one-time link code it gave you). The partner must confirm the code; the code is never stored. Read-only: Crossly never trades on it.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketDcmUnlink**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketdcmunlink) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/dcm/unlink | Unlink the partner exchange account from your account (or the firm sub-account &#x60;subaccountId&#x60;): Crossly stops reading it and its hedges leave portfolio margin.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketDealerApply**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketdealerapply) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/apply | Apply to be a dealer (ID check first). Ops review; approved dealers get raised limits and their bids show as dealer bids.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketDealerBid**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketdealerbid) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/bids | Place a standing dealer bid: a resting GTC/GTD limit buy, flagged as a dealer bid. Approved dealers only.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketDealerPool**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketdealerpool) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/pools | Open a curve pool on a key (approved dealers, ID checked): linear (delta in cents) or exponential (delta in bps), spot, fee, levels per side, units it may sell, and a buy budget from your custodian account. Its ladder goes up at once.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketDealerPoolClose**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketdealerpoolclose) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/pools/{id}/close | Close a curve pool: its legs come off the book; what filled stays filled.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketDealerPoolPreview**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketdealerpoolpreview) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/pools/preview | The bids and asks a set of curve settings would show, and why each side stops where it does. Places nothing.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketDeposit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketdeposit) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/deposits | Open a ship-in deposit for an ungraded catalogue item (&#x60;skuId&#x60;, &#x60;variantId&#x60;, &#x60;condition&#x60; DS / VNDS / Used). It is inbound until the vault receives and checks it, then stored in your name. Returns &#x60;{ vaultItem: { id, status } }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketDonation**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketdonation) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/donations | Give a unit to Crossly Cares (once the charity can receive; otherwise its gate message). Returns &#x60;{ id, markCents }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketEntity**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketentity) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/entities | Open an entity account: you become its admin and the custodian partner starts KYB (its own flow; Crossly keeps only its reference). Needs a verified ID and the custodian (503 names what is missing).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketEntityEcp**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketentityecp) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/ecp | Admin: record the entity&#39;s attestation that it is an eligible contract participant. Raising its caps is still an ops decision.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketEntityHalt**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketentityhalt) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/halts | Admin or risk: the firm kill switch. Halts the entity (no &#x60;subaccountId&#x60;) or one sub-account: open orders on it are cancelled and no new ones are placed until cleared.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketEntityHaltClear**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketentityhaltclear) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/halts/{haltId}/clear | Admin or risk: lift a firm halt.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketEntityInvitation**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketentityinvitation) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/invitation | Accept or decline an invitation (&#x60;accept&#x60;). Trading, admin and risk roles need a verified ID to accept.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketEntityKyb**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketentitykyb) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/kyb | Refresh the entity&#39;s KYB status from the custodian (and its onboarding link while KYB is open).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketEntityMember**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketentitymember) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/members | Admin: invite a Crossly user by email as admin, trader, risk, ops or viewer (a viewer can never place an order), with optional limits and sub-accounts.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketEntitySubaccount**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketentitysubaccount) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/subaccounts | Admin: open a sub-account once the custodian approved the entity; its limits are held to the entity&#39;s caps.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketEntitySubaccountClose**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketentitysubaccountclose) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/subaccounts/{subId}/close | Admin: close a sub-account that holds nothing and has no open orders.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketFollowCopyWatchAlertRead**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketfollowcopywatchalertread) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/follows/copy-watch/alerts/read | Mark every copy-watch alert read. Returns &#x60;{ marked }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketGift**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketgift) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/gifts | Give a unit in your vault to another Crossly user (by @username or email). It stays on its shelf; they have 7 days to accept. ID needed above $1,000 at the mark. Returns &#x60;{ id, expiresAt }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketGiftAccept**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketgiftaccept) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/gifts/{id}/accept | Accept a gift: the unit becomes yours in place. Returns &#x60;{ status }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketGiftCancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketgiftcancel) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/gifts/{id}/cancel | Cancel a gift you sent before it is accepted. Returns &#x60;{ status }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketGiftDecline**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketgiftdecline) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/gifts/{id}/decline | Decline a gift: the unit stays with the giver. Returns &#x60;{ status }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketGradeToVault**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketgradetovault) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/grade-to-vault | Start Grade to Vault on one item (&#x60;source&#x60; inventory or vault_unit). With &#x60;runId&#x60; the grading slot is booked at once (free; your saved card is charged only if the run locks). Nothing is listed until you confirm a price after the grade.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketGradeToVaultBook**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketgradetovaultbook) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/grade-to-vault/{id}/book | Book (or re-book) the grading slot of a flow still at \&quot;picked\&quot;. Answers 503 naming GRADING_HUB_ADDRESS when runs are not open.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketGradeToVaultCancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketgradetovaultcancel) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/grade-to-vault/{id}/cancel | Stop the flow before the card goes to the grader (still picked, or its slot only reserved: nothing was charged).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketGradeToVaultKeep**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketgradetovaultkeep) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/grade-to-vault/{id}/keep | Keep the graded unit in your vault without listing it. The flow ends; list it any time from the vault.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketGradeToVaultList**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketgradetovaultlist) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/grade-to-vault/{id}/list | Confirm the price for the grade the unit came back with (&#x60;confirm: true&#x60;, &#x60;gradeKey&#x60; must match the unit). Pre-trade risk, the price band and the ID line apply, then one shelf ask is placed on the Crossly order book.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketGradeToVaultShipped**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketgradetovaultshipped) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/grade-to-vault/{id}/shipped | You mailed the card to the grading hub (an inventory card, once its run locked).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketGrading**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketgrading) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/grading | Send a raw vault unit on the grading hub&#39;s shelf to a Grading Run (&#x60;runId&#x60;). It is held until it comes back, graded, to the same vault item. The slot is free; your saved card is charged only if the run locks.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketGradingPull**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketgradingpull) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/grading/{submissionId}/pull | Pull a vault unit out of its run before the run locks (nothing was charged). It goes back on the shelf.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketHallLoan**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarkethallloan) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/hall/loans | Lend a unit in your Crossly vault to the Hall of Grails: { vaultItemId, credit: &#39;name&#39; | &#39;anonymous&#39; }. A standing loan of display rights only: the unit goes on a plinth, under a plate naming you (or \&quot;a collector\&quot;), in every weekly exhibition whose category it fits. It never moves and stays yours to list, sell or withdraw. Earns reputation per exhibition shown; no cash. Resumes a loan you opted out of while it still shows.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketHallLoanCredit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarkethallloancredit) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/hall/loans/{id}/credit | Change how the plate on a lent unit&#39;s plinth names you, from now: { credit: &#39;name&#39; } (your display name) or { credit: &#39;anonymous&#39; } (\&quot;a collector\&quot;).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketHallLoanOptOut**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarkethallloanoptout) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/hall/loans/{id}/opt-out | Opt a Hall of Grails loan out, at any time. A unit on a plinth this week finishes this exhibition (status ending, showingUntil); otherwise the loan ends now (status ended).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketIdentityStart**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketidentitystart) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/identity/start | Start (or resume) the one-time ID check the money features and larger orders need. Returns &#x60;{ url }&#x60; to open, or &#x60;{ alreadyVerified: true }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketIndex**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketindex) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/indexes | Make an index of your own: 1 to 50 book keys, weighted by mark or equally. Up to 10 per account.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketIndexBuy**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketindexbuy) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/indexes/{slug}/buy | Buy the basket: one limit buy per key in the index for 1-5 whole units each, at most &#x60;maxBpsOverMark&#x60; over each key&#39;s mark, IOC or GTC. Every unit is a real item that lands in your vault. Idempotent on &#x60;clientBasketId&#x60;; returns a result per key.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketIndexComplaint**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketindexcomplaint) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/index-complaints | File a complaint about an index or the method (&#x60;slug&#x60; optional). Every complaint is answered. Returns &#x60;{ id, status, createdAt }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketIndexConsultationComment**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketindexconsultationcomment) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/index-consultations/{id}/comments | Comment on an open consultation (5 to 5,000 characters; up to 5 per person). Comments are published without your name. Returns &#x60;{ ok }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketInstantExit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketinstantexit) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/instant-exit | Sell a unit now at the quoted price: a sell IOC at &#x60;priceCents&#x60; through the pro engine. Refused, with the new quote, if the best bid dropped below it. Crossly never buys or funds it.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketJoint**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketjoint) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/joint | Open a joint vault (ID first): you hold full rights and accept the current joint terms. &#x60;invite&#x60; sends invitations (@username or email, each with view, trade or full rights). Returns &#x60;{ id, status, invited }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketJointClose**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketjointclose) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/close | Close the joint vault (full rights): open joint orders are cancelled and every unit goes back to its owner. Returns &#x60;{ status, unitsReturned }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketJointInvite**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketjointinvite) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/invite | Invite a Crossly user (full rights only). They have 14 days and show ID to join. Returns &#x60;{ userId, rights, expiresAt }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketJointMemberRemove**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketjointmemberremove) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/members/{userId}/remove | Remove a member (full rights), or leave when userId is you. Their units go back to their own account and their joint orders are cancelled. Returns &#x60;{ userId, status, unitsReturned, jointStatus }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketJointMemberRight**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketjointmemberright) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/members/{userId}/rights | Set a member rights to view, trade or full (full rights only; the vault always keeps a full member). Returns &#x60;{ userId, rights }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketJointRespond**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketjointrespond) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/respond | Accept (ID first; accepts the current joint terms) or decline an invitation. Returns &#x60;{ status }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketJointTerm**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketjointterm) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/terms | Accept the current joint terms (needed to trade after the terms version changes). Returns &#x60;{ termsVersion }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketJointTrade**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketjointtrade) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/trades | Place a limit order for the joint vault (trade or full rights, ID shown). A buy (bookKey) is placed on you, paid from your own Vault Markets account, and what it buys is booked to the joint vault. A sell (vaultItemId of a unit in it) is placed on the unit owner of record, who gets the proceeds; pre-trade checks run against you too. Returns &#x60;{ tradeId, side, bookKey, ownerUserId, order }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketJointTradeCancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketjointtradecancel) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/trades/{tradeId}/cancel | Cancel a working joint order (trade or full rights); what filled stays filled. Returns &#x60;{ tradeId, order }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketJointUnit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketjointunit) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/units | Put units you own into the joint vault (each stored and free). They keep you as owner of record and stay on the shelf. Returns &#x60;{ added, vaultItemIds }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketJointUnitRemove**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketjointunitremove) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/units/remove | Take units out of the joint vault, back to their owner own account (yours at any rights; anyone else with full rights). Returns &#x60;{ removed, vaultItemIds }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketLargeTraderReportFile**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketlargetraderreportfile) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/large-trader-reports/{id}/file | File (or amend, or refile) a large-trader report: why you hold it (purpose), who you act for, other accounts you control, whether you plan to add, and a statement of 20+ characters. Returns the report.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketLearnQuiz**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketlearnquiz) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/learn/{concept}/quiz | Answer a concept&#39;s quiz (&#x60;answers&#x60;: the chosen choice index per question). Graded on the server; 5 tries an hour.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketLiveStreamFeaturedLot**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketlivestreamfeaturedlot) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/live-streams/{id}/featured-lot | Feature an open lot on your live show (or &#x60;lotId: null&#x60; to take it off). Viewers hear LIVE_PIN_CHANGED. Returns &#x60;{ ok }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketLoan**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketloan) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/loans | Borrow against stored vault units: they are pledged (no listing, lending or withdrawal) and the market pool lends &#x60;amountCents&#x60; into your custodian account. Needs a verified ID.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketLoanRecall**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketloanrecall) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/loans/{id}/recall | Recall a lent unit: the short has 3 days to return one. Returns &#x60;{ recallDueAt }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketLoanUnit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketloanunit) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/loans/{id}/units | Pledge more stored units to a loan (answers a loan call, or makes room).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketLoanUnitRelease**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketloanunitrelease) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/loans/{id}/units/{vaultItemId}/release | Free one pledged unit, if what is owed still fits inside the other units&#39; limit.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketLoanUnitSell**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketloanunitsell) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/loans/{id}/units/{vaultItemId}/sell | Sell a pledged unit to repay its loan: it is listed at &#x60;priceCents&#x60; (at least the sale floor) and stays pledged until it sells; the proceeds repay the loan first and the rest is yours. Calling it again re-prices it.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketLocate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketlocate) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/locates | Hold up to &#x60;qty&#x60; lendable units of &#x60;bookKey&#x60; for 15 minutes so a short can open against them (&#x60;termDays&#x60; 30/90 for a term loan). Needs lending (VAULT_LENDING_TERMS_VERSION) and the ID check. Returns the locate with how many units it holds.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketLong**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketlong) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/longs | Buy &#x60;units&#x60; units (default 1; at most 25/10/3/1 by liquidity tier A/B/C/D) into your vault with leverage (up to 2x) from the market staking pool, paying up to &#x60;maxCents&#x60; each. A partial fill opens with the units bought and the rest is cancelled. Returns &#x60;{ positionId, matched, units }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketMidpoint**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketmidpoint) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/midpoint | A hidden block order that trades only at the firm-book midpoint, with another midpoint order of at least your minimum size.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketMidpointCancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketmidpointcancel) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/midpoint/{id}/cancel | Cancel a midpoint order.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketMmDmm**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketmmdmm) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/mm/dmm | Apply to be the designated market maker on a tier B or C key. Ops approves; one per key.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketMmLeave**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketmmleave) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/mm/{id}/leave | Leave a market-maker programme.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketMmLp**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketmmlp) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/mm/lp | Join a key as a Registered Liquidity Provider (ID needed). Meeting coverage earns the LP rates.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketMmMassCancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketmmmasscancel) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/mm/mass-cancel | Cancel every open order (one key, or only quotes) and every resting leg in one statement: nothing fills between the first cancel and the last.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketMmMmpReset**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketmmmmpreset) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/mm/mmp/reset | End a market-maker protection freeze early.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketOpeningWaitlist**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketopeningwaitlist) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/opening/{slug}/waitlist | Join a key&#39;s waitlist, optionally with a friend&#39;s invite code (&#x60;ref&#x60;). Inviting moves you up the queue; nobody is paid. Returns your place and your own code.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketP2pLoanCall**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketp2ploancall) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/loans/{id}/call | Lender: call the loan (once it is a day old). A refinancing auction runs for about 30 hours, the rate a new lender gets climbing to the ceiling; repaid or refinanced, you are paid in full; neither by the end, the unit moves to your vault.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketP2pLoanCancelCall**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketp2ploancancelcall) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/loans/{id}/cancel-call | Lender: take back your call before any other lender has taken the loan over. The refinancing auction stops and the loan carries on as before on its terms.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketP2pLoanRefiAuction**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketp2ploanrefiauction) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/loans/{id}/refi-auction | Borrower: look for a cheaper lender. A refinancing auction runs for &#x60;hours&#x60; (1-72, default 24); the rate a new lender gets climbs from &#x60;startAprBps&#x60; (default half the loan rate) to just under the loan rate now. The first lender to take it pays your lender off and the loan carries on at that lower rate. Nobody by the end: nothing changes.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketP2pLoanRefiAuctionCancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketp2ploanrefiauctioncancel) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/loans/{id}/refi-auction/cancel | Borrower: stop looking for a cheaper lender before anyone has taken the loan over. The loan carries on unchanged.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketP2pLoanRefinance**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketp2ploanrefinance) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/loans/{id}/refinance | Take over a loan in a refinancing auction (a lender call or a borrower search for a cheaper lender): you pay the old lender everything owed now (refused above &#x60;maxPayoffCents&#x60;), which becomes your loan to the same borrower at the auction rate now. Needs a verified ID, an active account and the current lending terms. Pass &#x60;requestId&#x60; to make a retry safe.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketP2pLoanRepay**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketp2ploanrepay) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/loans/{id}/repay | Borrower: repay from your custodian account to the lender. Without &#x60;amountCents&#x60;, in full (principal plus interest to the second; the unit is free once the custodian has it). With &#x60;amountCents&#x60; below what is owed, a part payment: interest first, then principal, at least the interest owed now and at least $1; the loan stays open on the principal left. Pass &#x60;requestId&#x60; to make a retry safe.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketP2pOffer**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketp2poffer) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/offers | Offer to lend: &#x60;principalCents&#x60; per loan at &#x60;aprBps&#x60; a year against an item (&#x60;bookKey&#x60;) or one unit (&#x60;vaultItemId&#x60;), for up to &#x60;maxLoans&#x60; loans, lasting &#x60;expiresInDays&#x60; (default 90). Nothing is held: the custodian pays from your own account when a borrower takes it. Needs a verified ID, an active account and the current lending terms.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketP2pOfferCancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketp2poffercancel) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/offers/{id}/cancel | Withdraw one of your open offers. Loans already taken from it carry on.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketP2pOfferTake**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketp2poffertake) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/offers/{id}/take | Borrow: take an offer against one of your stored vault units (&#x60;vaultItemId&#x60;). The unit is pledged (no listing, lending or withdrawal) and the principal is paid into your custodian account. Interest accrues by the second; repay any time. Pass &#x60;requestId&#x60; to make a retry safe.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketPaperMissionMarkCheck**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketpapermissionmarkcheck) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/paper/missions/mark-check | Answer the \&quot;Read the mark\&quot; mission: the quality letter (A, B or C) you read on a market page, checked against the stored mark. One answer per market, 10 a day. Returns &#x60;{ bookKey, answer, correct, quality, mission, next }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketPaperOrder**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketpaperorder) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/paper/orders | Place a paper order (play money only). It takes visible real liquidity now at book prices with the taker fee simulated; a GTC limit rests the rest with a queue-position estimate, filled later as the real tape reaches it. Sells only paper units you hold (no paper shorts). Never touches the real book. Returns &#x60;{ order, fills }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketPaperOrderCancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketpaperordercancel) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/paper/orders/{id}/cancel | Cancel a working paper order: the play cash or paper units it held come back. Returns &#x60;{ order }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketPaperReset**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketpaperreset) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/paper/reset | Reset your paper account to $10,000 of play money: working paper orders are cancelled, paper positions cleared, and your leaderboard week starts again. Once a day.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketPartnerApply**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketpartnerapply) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/partners/apply | Apply as a Vault Drop-off Partner (a card shop that takes walk-ins for the vault). Needs the one-time ID check; ops approve.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketPartnerBatch**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketpartnerbatch) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/partners/batches | An approved partner sends walk-ins in under its code: each item (catalogue variant, condition, grade, photos) becomes an inbound vault unit; the box gets one prepaid label. The partner earns a capped share of Crossly&#39;s fee on each unit&#39;s first sale.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketPoolStake**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketpoolstake) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/pool/stakes | Stake CBX you hold at the custodian into the market pool (base units, as a string). Returns &#x60;{ stakeId, shares }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketPoolStakeUnstake**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketpoolstakeunstake) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/pool/stakes/{id}/unstake | Unstake: paid out after the 7-day cooldown. Returns &#x60;{ cooldownUntil }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketPortfolioMarginEnroll**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketportfoliomarginenroll) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/portfolio-margin/enroll | Admin: put a firm sub-account under portfolio margin. Needs VAULT_LEVERAGE_VENUE, your ID, an ECP-attested entity that passed KYB, and the minimum equity. Its positions are then margined on the whole account, recalculated every 5 minutes; calls and auctions are unchanged. Audited.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketPortfolioMarginLeave**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketportfoliomarginleave) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/portfolio-margin/leave | Admin: take a firm sub-account off portfolio margin. Each position is then held to its own tier floors again and may be called at once. Audited.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketPortfolioMarginWhatIf**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketportfoliomarginwhatif) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/portfolio-margin/what-if | The portfolio margin requirement of a firm sub-account before and after a trade (&#x60;bookKey&#x60;, &#x60;side&#x60; buy or sell, &#x60;units&#x60;), at the Crossly mark with fees left out. Changes nothing.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketPositionCancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketpositioncancel) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/positions/{id}/cancel | Cancel the working order: an unfilled opening order cancels the position; an unfilled cover or close leaves it open. Returns &#x60;{ ok }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketPositionClose**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketpositionclose) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/positions/{id}/close | Close a leveraged long: all its units, or &#x60;units&#x60; of them, each sold for at least &#x60;minCents&#x60;; each sale repays the pool first.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketPositionCollateral**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketpositioncollateral) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/positions/{id}/collateral | Answer a margin call: a short posts more collateral (USD, or CBX at half value); a leveraged long pays down its pool loan. Returns the position.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketPositionCover**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketpositioncover) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/positions/{id}/cover | Cover a short: all its units, or &#x60;units&#x60; of them, each buying any unit of the key into the vault for up to &#x60;maxCents&#x60;; each goes back to its lender.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketPriceWidget**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketpricewidget) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/price-widgets | Make a price widget for one vault market (&#x60;kind&#x60; mark + &#x60;bookKey&#x60;) or one public index (&#x60;kind&#x60; index + &#x60;indexSlug&#x60;): range, layout (compact or chart), theme (auto, light, dark), optionally your own affiliate code for the link back and the sites allowed to show it (empty &#x3D; any). Returns the widget with its snippets.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketPriceWidgetPreview**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketpricewidgetpreview) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/price-widgets/preview | What a widget would show before you save it: &#x60;view&#x60; (value, move, quality, n, as-of, link back) and &#x60;html&#x60;, the exact page the iframe serves. Nothing is stored.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketProBracket**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketprobracket) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/pro/brackets | A buy order with a take-profit and/or stop-loss attached; the exits arm as an OCO pair for what the entry fills.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketProLinkCancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketprolinkcancel) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/pro/links/{id}/cancel | Cancel a linked order: its open child orders come off; a bracket&#39;s entry stays.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketProOco**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketprooco) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/pro/oco | Two orders on one key, one cancels the other: when one fills or its stop triggers, the other is cancelled (a partial fill shrinks it).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketProOrder**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketproorder) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/pro/orders | Place an order on a vault book: limit, market (protected, IOC), stop or stop-limit; GTC, GTD, IOC or FOK; post-only; an iceberg &#x60;displayQuantity&#x60;; a TWAP or VWAP algo over a window. Idempotent on &#x60;clientOrderId&#x60;. &#x60;subaccountId&#x60; trades for a firm sub-account you may trade (your role, its KYB, its limits, no firm halt; buys pay from its custodian account and land in it).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketProOrderCancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketproordercancel) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/pro/orders/{id}/cancel | Cancel an order; what filled stays filled.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketProOrderCancelAll**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketproordercancelall) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/pro/orders/cancel-all | Cancel every open order, or every one on a book key. Returns &#x60;{ cancelled }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketProOrderReplace**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketproorderreplace) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/pro/orders/{id}/replace | Amend an order&#39;s price, stop or quantity (cancel-replace: it re-queues at its new price).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketProQuote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketproquote) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/pro/quotes | Market makers: set a two-sided post-only quote on a book key (replaces the last). With cancel-on-disconnect (default) heartbeat every 30 s or it is pulled.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketProQuoteHeartbeat**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketproquoteheartbeat) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/pro/quotes/heartbeat | Keep your quotes alive (cancel-on-disconnect). Returns &#x60;{ quotes, at }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketProTrailingStop**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketprotrailingstop) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/pro/trailing-stops | A trailing stop: its stop follows the best price since it was placed, &#x60;trailBps&#x60; or &#x60;trailCents&#x60; behind (under the high for a sell, over the low for a buy).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketProfileFollow**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketprofilefollow) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/profiles/{handle}/follow | Follow someone with a public vault: their new vault buys appear in your feed. No copy trading.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketRecurring**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketrecurring) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/recurring | Start a recurring buy: one whole unit of a key every week, two weeks or month, at no more than &#x60;maxPriceCents&#x60; and/or the mark plus &#x60;capBpsOverMark&#x60;; card (with an optional backup) or custodian; optional auto-lend.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketRfq**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketrfq) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/rfq | Ask for a quote on a block: dealers, the key&#39;s DMM and LPs, and holders are told, anonymously. Answers are firm for 60 s. Your limit is never shown.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketRfqAccept**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketrfqaccept) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/rfq/{id}/accept | Take one answer: it fills as a block through the normal fill path (fees by role, self-trade prevention, halts, LULD).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketRfqCancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketrfqcancel) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/rfq/{id}/cancel | Cancel your open quote request.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketRfqRespond**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketrfqrespond) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/rfq/{id}/respond | Answer a quote request for the full quantity with a firm price, good for 60 s. Selling needs the units free in your vault; buying pays from your Vault Markets custodian account.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketRfqResponseWithdraw**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketrfqresponsewithdraw) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/rfq/responses/{id}/withdraw | Withdraw your live answer to a quote request.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketSetGoal**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketsetgoal) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/set-goals | Start a goal on a catalogue set (category, brandSlug, modelSlug from the set search), optionally with a target: one condition, and any grade, ungraded, or one exact grade. One live goal per set.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketSetGoalBasket**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketsetgoalbasket) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/set-goals/{id}/basket | Place the \&quot;complete my set\&quot; basket bid: one GTC limit buy per missing piece, under a hard budget, paid by one of your saved cards or your custodian account. Every leg passes pre-trade risk and the ID line like any order. Idempotent on clientBasketId. One working basket per goal; its legs are cancelled once the set is complete.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketSetGoalBasketCancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketsetgoalbasketcancel) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/set-goals/{id}/basket/cancel | Cancel the goal working basket bid: every working leg comes off the book; what already filled stays in your vault.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketShareCard**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketsharecard) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/share-cards | Make a verified share card of a position, a vault unit, a public index or your portfolio (&#x60;kind&#x60;, &#x60;refId&#x60;). The % on it is the price move since your real fill; amounts stay hidden unless &#x60;showAmounts&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketShort**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketshort) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/shorts | Short a book key: borrow &#x60;units&#x60; lent units (default 1; at most 25/10/3/1 by liquidity tier A/B/C/D) and sell each at &#x60;limitCents&#x60; or better, posting collateral for all of them (50% of the mark each; CBX counts at half, up to 30%). All units are borrowed or none unless &#x60;allowPartial&#x60;. A partial sale opens with the units sold. Returns &#x60;{ positionId, matched, units }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketSupplyBatchLabel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketsupplybatchlabel) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/supply/batches/{id}/label | Buy a box&#39;s prepaid label again after it failed (for example once a ship-from address is saved). A box that has a label keeps it.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketSupplyInvite**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketsupplyinvite) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/supply/invites/{id} | Accept or decline a Market Open invite (&#x60;accept&#x60;).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketSupplySend**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketsupplysend) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/supply/send | Send inventory items matched to a catalogue variant to the vault as one box: each becomes an inbound vault unit, and the box gets one prepaid inbound label (a PDF to print). Optional: auto-list each unit in the vault book at a share of the mark once stored, and lend when idle. Items that cannot go say why. Crosslisted listings stay live.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketTerm**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketterm) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/terms | Accept the current lending terms (needed to lend or short). Returns &#x60;{ termsVersion }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketTodClaimObject**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarkettodclaimobject) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/tod/claims/{id}/object | Stop a transfer-on-death claim made on your own account. Returns &#x60;{ id, status, estateUnlocked }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketTodMyClaim**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarkettodmyclaim) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/tod/my-claims | Start a claim as a named beneficiary (ID first), naming the account holder by @username or email. It stays a draft until you add documents and submit. Returns &#x60;{ id, status, existing }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketTodMyClaimDocument**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarkettodmyclaimdocument) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/tod/my-claims/{id}/documents | Add one document to a draft claim (or one ops asked more of): kind, filename and the file as base64 (PDF, JPEG or PNG, up to 8 MB). Kept privately for ops review. Returns &#x60;{ id, kind, label, filename, bytes }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketTodMyClaimSubmit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarkettodmyclaimsubmit) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/tod/my-claims/{id}/submit | Send a claim for ops review (it needs a death certificate). The account holder is told and can stop it. Returns &#x60;{ id, status }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketTodMyClaimWithdraw**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarkettodmyclaimwithdraw) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/tod/my-claims/{id}/withdraw | Withdraw a claim before it is released. Returns &#x60;{ id, status }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketTodTerm**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarkettodterm) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/tod/terms | Accept the current transfer-on-death terms. Returns &#x60;{ termsVersion }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketUnitEvidenceScan**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketunitevidencescan) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/units/{id}/evidence/scans | Add a finished scan of your own (&#x60;captureId&#x60;, of the same catalogue item) to your vault unit. Shown as added by the owner, not as intake evidence.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketUnitSellNow**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketunitsellnow) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/units/{id}/sell-now | Sell one unit of this item now into the best bid (Instant Exit: an immediate-or-cancel pro order), never below &#x60;minCents&#x60;. Returns &#x60;{ status: filled | missed, filledCents, quote, order }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketValuationStatement**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketvaluationstatement) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/valuation-statements | Issue an insurance or appraisal statement for a day: frozen with the sha256 of its record, sealed in the Crossly audit chain, with a public verify link. preparedFor and policyRef are printed on it and never shown on verify. Moves no money.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketWatchlist**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketwatchlist) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/watchlist | Watch a book key. Returns &#x60;{ bookKey, watching }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultMarketWidgetToken**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultmarketwidgettoken) | **Post** /v1/vault-markets/widget/tokens | Mint a widget token for one phone (&#x60;deviceName&#x60;, &#x60;installId&#x60;); minting again with the same pair replaces that phone token. The token reads the widget summary and nothing else, and is returned once. At most five phones.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultReceiptTokenConfirm**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultreceipttokenconfirm) | **Post** /v1/vault/receipt-tokens/{id}/confirm | After your wallet sent the export: read the chain and mark the token live once its one token exists. Pass the &#x60;signature&#x60; your wallet returned to record it (kept only if it landed).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultReceiptTokenExport**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultreceipttokenexport) | **Post** /v1/vault/receipt-tokens/export | Send one unit (&#x60;vaultItemId&#x60;) warehouse receipt to one of your verified Solana wallets (&#x60;wallet&#x60;) as a token, accepting the current terms (&#x60;acceptTerms&#x60;). Returns a base64 transaction already signed by the new mint and the receipts authority: your wallet pays the rent and must sign and send it within about two minutes, then call confirm. Nothing is minted until it does. Needs a verified ID; the unit must be stored, unpledged, not lent and not for sale.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultReceiptTokenRedeem**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultreceipttokenredeem) | **Post** /v1/vault/receipt-tokens/{id}/redeem | Redeem a receipt token held in one of your verified Solana wallets (&#x60;wallet&#x60;): returns an unsigned base64 burn your wallet signs and sends (it also closes the token account and returns its rent to you). Once it lands, call redeem/confirm and the unit is yours in the vault. Refused while the token is frozen.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultReceiptTokenRedeemConfirm**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultreceipttokenredeemconfirm) | **Post** /v1/vault/receipt-tokens/{id}/redeem/confirm | After your wallet sent the redeem: read the chain; once the token is burned the unit moves into your vault (if it was not yours already). Pass the &#x60;signature&#x60; to record it.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**CreateVaultReceiptTokenWalletAllow**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#createvaultreceipttokenwalletallow) | **Post** /v1/vault/receipt-tokens/wallets/allow | Put one of your verified Solana wallets (&#x60;wallet&#x60;) on the receipt-token allow-list so it can hold and receive receipt tokens. Returns a base64 transaction already signed by the receipts authority: the wallet pays the small rent (about 0.001 SOL) and must sign and send it within about two minutes. Needs a verified ID and a wallet that passes the sanctions screen.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketAgentWatch**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketagentwatch) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/agent/watches/{id} | Cancel a live price watch. Returns the watch.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketAlert**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketalert) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/alerts/{id} | Delete a price alert. Returns &#x60;{ ok }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketBullionFamilyPeg**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketbullionfamilypeg) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/bullion/family-pegs/{id} | Stop a family-wide quote: every leg order comes off the book; what filled stays filled.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketBullionPeg**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketbullionpeg) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/bullion/pegs/{id} | Stop a spot-pegged quote: its resting order comes off the book; what filled stays filled.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketDealerBand**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketdealerband) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/bands | Stop a band on a key; its standing bid comes off.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketEntityMember**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketentitymember) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/members/{memberId} | Admin: remove a member (or leave, yourself). An entity always keeps one admin.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketFollowCopyWatch**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketfollowcopywatch) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/follows/copy-watch/{handle} | Stop copy-watching a collector.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketIndex**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketindex) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/indexes/{slug} | Delete an index of your own. Returns &#x60;{ ok }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketLocate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketlocate) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/locates/{id} | Release a live locate before it lapses.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketOpeningWaitlist**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketopeningwaitlist) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/opening/{slug}/waitlist | Leave a key&#39;s waitlist.
