*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketPriceWidget**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketpricewidget) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/price-widgets/{id} | Delete a widget. Pages that still paste it show that it is no longer available. Returns &#x60;{ id, archived }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketProQuote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketproquote) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/pro/quotes | Pull your quote on a book key, or every quote. Returns &#x60;{ pulled }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketProfileFollow**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketprofilefollow) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/profiles/{handle}/follow | Stop following someone.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketRecurring**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketrecurring) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/recurring/{id} | Stop a recurring buy for good; its history stays.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketSetGoal**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketsetgoal) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/set-goals/{id} | Remove a set goal. A working basket bid is cancelled first; what filled stays in your vault.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketShareCard**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketsharecard) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/share-cards/{id} | Take a share card down: its link and image stop working. Returns &#x60;{ ok }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketSupplyRoundUp**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketsupplyroundup) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/supply/round-up | Stop the round-up (its history stays; an armed buy is cancelled).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketUnitEvidence**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketunitevidence) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/units/{id}/evidence/{evidenceId} | Take back a scan you added to your vault unit (intake evidence stays).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketWatchlist**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketwatchlist) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/watchlist | Stop watching a book key (&#x60;?bookKey&#x3D;&#x60;). Returns &#x60;{ bookKey, watching }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketWidgetToken**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketwidgettoken) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/widget/tokens | Turn the widget off on every phone: revokes all your widget tokens. Returns &#x60;{ revoked }&#x60;, how many.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**DeleteVaultMarketWidgetTokenById**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#deletevaultmarketwidgettokenbyid) | **Delete** /v1/vault-markets/widget/tokens/{id} | Turn the widget off on one phone: revokes that widget token at once. Only a widget token can be revoked here.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultAttestation**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultattestation) | **Get** /v1/vault/attestations | Vault attestations: independent physical counts, the insurance certificate (with a warning when insured value in the vault exceeds its aggregate or per-occurrence limit), SOC 1 Type II and SOC 2 reports. Each carries its signed document.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketAccount**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketaccount) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/account | Which Vault Markets registrations are live (and the env each waits on), your custodian account, limits and balances.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketAdl**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketadl) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/adl | The insurance fund (booked and free to draw), the most one event may draw before auto-deleveraging (ADL) starts, the loss waterfall in order, and the latest ADL events (no user or position ids). Money in cents.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketAdlPosition**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketadlposition) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/adl/positions/{id} | One of your positions with its ADL lights (0 once it has ended).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketAdvanceQuote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketadvancequote) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/advances/quote | What an advance on a vault unit for sale could be: up to half of the lower of its mark and asking price, free for 60 days.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketAgentDraft**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketagentdraft) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/agent/drafts/{id} | One drafted order: item, side, units, limit (cents), the book when drafted, status, and the orderBody that places it.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketAlertPref**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketalertpref) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/alert-prefs | Your vault alert settings: every kind (price, fill, margin_call, adl, p2p_loan, auction, market_open, receipt_token, set_goal, follow, account), every channel (push, telegram, terminal, email, webhook) with whether it is set up, the full on/off grid, the locked switches (a critical kind always pushes) and quiet hours.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketAuctionLot**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketauctionlot) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/auctions/lots/{id} | One lot: the price showing, the next minimum bid, whether a reserve exists and is met, the end (extended by late bids), the bid history and your standing.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketAuctionMine**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketauctionmine) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/auctions/mine | Lots you consigned (with your reserve) and lots you bid on (with your maximum and whether you lead).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketBlock**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketblock) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/blocks/{orderId} | One block order: its order, plan lines (planned and allocated units, average price, cash share, status).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketBullionFor**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketbullionfor) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/bullion/for | The bullion key families one book key belongs to, with spot for its metal as a labelled reference and its melt value at that spot. Empty families when the item is not keyed bullion.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketBullionKey**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketbullionkey) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/bullion/keys/{slug} | One bullion key family: its catalogue products, every live book key with best bid, best ask, vault units and its mark (from the book only), live pegged quote counts, and spot as a labelled reference.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketBullionPegPreview**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketbullionpegpreview) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/bullion/pegs/preview | The price a spot-pegged quote would rest at now (spot plus a premium in cents or basis points, clamped to floor and cap). No price when spot is stale or missing: such a quote waits off the book.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketCall**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketcall) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/calls/{bookKey} | Recent call-auction uncrosses on a book key: kind (open, reopen, periodic, daily, buy-in, sell-off), clearing price in cents and units.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketCapacity**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketcapacity) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/capacity | Capacity from the book: &#x60;bookKey&#x60; (+ &#x60;units&#x60;) gives ADV, depth within ±5% of the mark, vault stock, lendable units and days to liquidate at 20% of ADV; &#x60;index&#x60; + &#x60;notional&#x60; (cents) gives how much of the basket the asks fill now and the days the rest needs.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketChannel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketchannel) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/channels | Your Vault Markets channel switches: Telegram alerts and digest, the daily cap on vault nudges by push (default 5), whether a Telegram chat is linked, and how many nudges went out today.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketCompare**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketcompare) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/compare | Up to 5 book keys and/or index slugs (&#x60;items&#x60;, comma-separated) as daily series on the same days, each rebased to 100.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketConfirmation**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketconfirmation) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/confirmations/{tradeId} | One trade confirmation.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketConfirmationPdf**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketconfirmationpdf) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/confirmations/{tradeId}/pdf | One trade confirmation as a PDF.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketCorrelation**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketcorrelation) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/correlations | The correlation matrix of the sector indexes&#39; weekly returns over &#x60;weeks&#x60; (default 52), pairwise-complete, with n per pair; a pair under 26 shared weeks has no number.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketCreatorAttribution**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketcreatorattribution) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/creators/attribution | The creator who brought you to Vault Markets, when one did: when it started and ends, whether it still earns, and the disclosure. Your prices and fees are the same either way.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketCreatorDashboard**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketcreatordashboard) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/creators/dashboard | For creators: whether you joined the programme, each of your codes with traders attributed and still earning (counts only), shares accrued, paid and voided (cents) and the latest shares.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketCreatorProgramme**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketcreatorprogramme) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/creators/programme | The Vault Markets creator programme: the share of the fee Crossly keeps that a creator earns, the per-fill and lifetime caps (cents), the attribution window in days, the 2% net floor it sits under, and the disclosure words.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketCreatorRoom**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketcreatorroom) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/creators/room | For creators: your vault shelf room (applied, verified, rejected or paused) and whether your public vault profile is on, which the room needs.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketCredit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketcredit) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/credit/{id} | One loan or advance with its pledged units and its money log (draws, interest, repayments, sale proceeds).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketDcm**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketdcm) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/dcm | The partner exchange (DCM) that lists and clears futures and options on Crossly indexes (needs VAULT_INDEX_DCM + VAULT_INDEX_DCM_API_URL + VAULT_INDEX_DCM_API_KEY), and its contracts as last read: perps, futures and options with the index each settles on, multiplier, tick, expiry and strike. Read-only: Crossly is the licensor and data provider, never the venue.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketDcmContract**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketdcmcontract) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/dcm/contracts/{symbol} | One partner contract: its spec, its funding prints (newest first; the partner computes and settles funding), annualised funding over the last 21 prints, and the Crossly index level it settles on.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketDcmContractBook**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketdcmcontractbook) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/dcm/contracts/{symbol}/book | The partner exchange live order book for a contract (cached 5 seconds), with mark, index, last, open interest, 24 h volume, spread in bps and the basis of its mark over the Crossly index in bps. Prices are index points.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketDcmOptionsInput**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketdcmoptionsinput) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/dcm/options-inputs/{slug} | Options inputs for an index (indexes only, never a single item): Crossly realised volatility (raw and Geltner-desmoothed, from weekly returns), the forward (the partner future mark, else the Crossly close), Black-76 greeks on a strike x expiry grid and on every option the partner lists on the index. Model inputs, not quotes.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketDcmPosition**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketdcmposition) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/dcm/positions | The partner exchange account linked to your account (or to the firm sub-account &#x60;subaccountId&#x60;) and its positions, read-only (cached 15 seconds); index futures on category indexes count as portfolio-margin hedges for that account: contracts, side, entry and mark price, unrealised P&amp;L and margin in cents, liquidation price and notional in cents. Not linked: &#x60;linked: false&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketDealer**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketdealer) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/dealer | Your dealer status (applied, approved, revoked) and your Instant Exit bands.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketDealerPool**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketdealerpool) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/pools/{id} | One of your curve pools, with its ladder and live legs.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketDigestPreview**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketdigestpreview) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/digest/preview | This week&#39;s vault digest as it would go out: units at the display marks (unmarked units counted, not valued), the like-for-like change from a week ago, offers on your units, watched keys that got a first mark, and one Learn card.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketEntity**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketentity) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/entities | The entities (funds, LLCs, trusts, SPVs) you belong to, and invitations waiting for you.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketEntityById**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketentitybyid) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id} | One entity: members and roles, sub-accounts with their limits, firm halts, caps.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketEstimate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketestimate) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/estimates/{bookKey} | The Crossly Estimate for a book key — a model, not a price. Method (own trades, index-adjusted last trade, grade ratio), cents with 68% and 95% bands, High/Medium/Low from the walk-forward backtest&#39;s FSD, and n. Hidden (&#x60;available: false&#x60;) under 50 backtested trades or when FSD &gt; 0.30. Never used for margin, loans or settlement.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketExecutionQuality**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketexecutionquality) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/execution-quality | The quarterly execution-quality report (Rule 605 style): fill rates, speed, effective vs quoted spread and shortfall by order type and size, and the busiest keys. &#x60;quarter&#x60; like 2026-Q4; the current quarter is provisional.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketFeeQuote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketfeequote) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/fees/quote | Your fee for an order at a price: as maker (the order rests) and as taker (it fills at once), with the programme that sets your rate and any Crossly One trim. A buyer&#39;s fee is added to the price; a seller&#39;s comes out of the proceeds.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketFeeSchedule**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketfeeschedule) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/fees/schedule | The fee schedule by role: maker and taker rates for retail, 30-day volume, Registered LPs and DMMs, the CBX discount, the referral share and the 2% net floor Crossly always keeps.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketFollowSharing**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketfollowsharing) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/follows/sharing | Your sharing switches: the public vault profile, new buys, units sent in, sales, Hall loans, bids and asks for copy-watchers, and the leaderboards. All off until you turn them on.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketGemRate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketgemrate) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/gem-rate/{bookKey} | The Crossly gem rate: of cards graded through Crossly, the share that came back at each grader&#39;s gem grade, for this item and its category, with a Wilson 95% band. 10 returns per grader or no number. Not a grader population report.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketGradeToVault**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketgradetovault) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/grade-to-vault/{id} | One Grade to Vault flow, brought up to date with its submission and unit.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketGradeToVaultCandidate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketgradetovaultcandidate) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/grade-to-vault/candidates | What you can start Grade to Vault on: raw inventory matched to a catalogue item, and raw vault units on the grading hub shelf; plus whether Grading Runs are open.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketGradingSignal**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketgradingsignal) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/grading-signal | For a raw book key: the marks of the same item&#39;s graded keys, the spread over raw, grading cost per grader, the margin tier grading lifts it to, your raw vault units it pays to grade, and where to act.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketHallLoan**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarkethallloan) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/hall/loans | Your Hall of Grails desk: this week&#39;s exhibition, the rules (maxLive loans, shownPerHolder per exhibition, repPoints per exhibition shown), reputation (exhibitions finished), your loans (status active, ending or ended; onShowNow, showingUntil, exhibitions, the next exhibition it fits, the Lent to the Hall badge) and the vault units you could lend (canLend, or the blocker in words, and the next exhibition each fits). Display only.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketHealth**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarkethealth) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/health | Portfolio health of your vault holdings at Crossly marks: concentration by single item, category, grade and grader (shares, HHI on 0 to 10000, its band and effective number of holdings), value by liquidity tier, the stress grid (each category and everything down 10, 20 and 30 percent), value by mark quality, plain observations, and the held items a what-if can run on. Information only, not advice.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketHealthWhatIf**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarkethealthwhatif) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/health/what-if | What if one held item moved by moveBps (-10000 to 10000 basis points of its mark): the item, your vault total, the item and its category share of the vault, and the single-item HHI, before and after. A scenario on the mark, not a price you could sell at.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketHomeUnit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarkethomeunit) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/home/units | \&quot;Show my vault\&quot;: the vault units you hold, the ones standing in your Crossly World home first. Each: vaultItemId, title, grade, imageUrl, bookKey (its market), inHome, visitors (visitors to your home see it). Also your home (slug, privacy, path), max units one home shows, shown, visitorsSee and a note on who sees what. Your home is made on first read. No prices.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketIndex**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketindex) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/indexes/{slug} | One index with its daily closes (oldest first) and its level now.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketIndexCalendar**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketindexcalendar) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/index-calendar | The next four quarterly reviews: reference day (2nd Friday of Mar/Jun/Sep/Dec), announcement day, last close under the old membership and the effective day; June and December also review the Blue-chip 50.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketIndexConsultation**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketindexconsultation) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/index-consultations/{id} | One consultation with its proposal, versions, dates, outcome and the public comments.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketIndexFeed**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketindexfeed) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/index-feed/{slug} | The licensed index feed for one built-in index (needs VAULT_INDEX_DCM + VAULT_INDEX_FEED_SECRET): the level now (intraday from A/B marks, or the last close below 50% constituent coverage), the last close, the frozen 7-day TWAP settlement value and every constituent with its mark source, quality and trade count. Signed: &#x60;x-crossly-signature: t&#x3D;&lt;ms&gt;,v1&#x3D;&lt;hex HMAC-SHA256(secret, t + \&quot;.\&quot; + body)&gt;&#x60;. Every read is logged for the licence. Crossly is the licensor and data feed only.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketIndexFeedFunding**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketindexfeedfunding) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/index-feed/{slug}/funding | Reference funding for a perpetual-style future on the index: your premium &#x60;premiumBps&#x60; (TWAP of (impact mid - index)/index) through F &#x3D; P + clamp(I - P, ±0.05%) per 8 h, |F| capped at 0.5%/day, with the index level it is relative to. The partner DCM computes and settles funding; this is the reference math.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketIndexFeedSettlement**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketindexfeedsettlement) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/index-feed/{slug}/settlement | The frozen 7-day TWAP settlement series for a licensed index (written once a day, never restated), with the closes behind each value. Signed like the feed.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketIndexLevel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketindexlevel) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/indexes/{slug}/levels | An index&#39;s daily closes (up to 5 years), oldest first.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketIndexLicenceUsage**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketindexlicenceusage) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/index-licence/usage | Your licensed index feed reads over &#x60;days&#x60;, per index and endpoint (licence-usage report).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketIndexMethodology**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketindexmethodology) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/index-methodology | The index method in machine-readable form: version (semver), inputs, weightings and caps, the family and its screens, the engines and their publication gate, revisions and the settlement series, the calendar, governance and limitations.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketInstantExit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketinstantexit) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/instant-exit | The Instant Exit quote for one vault unit you hold: the best resting bid on its book key (anyone&#39;s, dealers&#39; included), its share of the mark, the bid depth, and whether it can be taken now.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketJoint**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketjoint) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/joint | Your joint vaults and the invitations waiting for you, with the gate (open, missing env), the joint terms in words and what each right allows. A JointVaultsHome object, not a collection.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketJointById**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketjointbyid) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id} | One joint vault (active members only): members and rights, the units in it at the mark with each owner of record, totals, recent joint trades, and the activity from the audit trail.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketKeyStat**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketkeystat) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/key-stats/{bookKey} | Everything derived about one book key in one read: the Crossly Estimate, the liquidity score, risk statistics and the gem rate. A part that cannot be read is &#x60;{ error }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketLearn**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketlearn) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/learn | The Learn hub: each concept with whether you passed its quiz. Passing earns cosmetic pins only.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketLearnByConcept**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketlearnbyconcept) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/learn/{concept} | One Learn concept: its sections and quiz questions (no answers).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketLendingElection**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketlendingelection) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/lending/election | Your lender election: &#x60;returnInKind&#x60; (shorts post 175% collateral, a 14-day buy-in, never cash-settled) and &#x60;allowTerm&#x60; (your units may go out on 30/90-day fixed-rate term loans, not recallable before term).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketLendingProgramme**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketlendingprogramme) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/lending/programme | Your lender programme report: lendable units, units out, utilisation, fee revenue (accrued / paid), term loans and return-in-kind loans in force, and each loan&#39;s rate against the key&#39;s rate today.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketLendingStatement**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketlendingstatement) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/lending/statement | Your daily collateral statement as a lender (today live, or &#x60;day&#x60;): per open loan the mark and its quality, fees owed to you, the short&#39;s collateral share at the custodian and its coverage against 105% of mark + fees. A unit without a mark usable for risk shows no coverage number.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketLiquidity**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketliquidity) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/liquidity/{bookKey} | The liquidity score (0-100) of a book key and every component behind it: trades in 30/90 days, distinct counterparties, median days between trades, time-weighted quoted spread, two-sided share and depth within 5% (hourly book snapshots), Amihud and Roll. Each component is a percentile within the category. No score under the data threshold.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketLoanQuote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketloanquote) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/loans/quote | How much you can borrow against vault units (&#x60;vaultItemIds&#x60;, comma-separated; none &#x3D; every stored unit you hold): each unit&#39;s tier, mark and limit, the pool&#39;s rate and yours.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketLoanUnitSaleFloor**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketloanunitsalefloor) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/loans/{id}/units/{vaultItemId}/sale-floor | The lowest price a pledged unit can be sold at to repay its loan: after the sale (less the vault fee and storage) what is still owed must fit inside the other units&#39; limit.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketLocate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketlocate) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/locates | A locate quote: how many units of &#x60;key&#x60; (a book key) you could borrow now (up to &#x60;qty&#x60;), the indicative rate (APR bps; &#x60;termDays&#x60; 30 or 90 for a fixed-rate term loan), and the collateral they need (150%, or 175% for units whose lenders elected return in kind only). Holds nothing; POST to hold.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketMark**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketmark) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/marks/{bookKey} | One book key mark with everything behind it: the price in cents, its quality (A trade-anchored, B firm two-sided, C indicative, none), its confidence in bps and the band in cents, when it was computed, whether risk may act on it (judged for margin), the related key it was implied from (impliedFrom: a grade, the model median of the other sizes, or an index; null when none), every stored input, and the learned relations it may be implied from.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketMarket**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketmarket) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/market | One book key (variantId:condition:gradeKey): its mark and history, lending depth and borrow rate, open interest and live auctions.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketMarketModel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketmarketmodel) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/market-model/{bookKey} | How a book key trades: its liquidity tier (A continuous, B call auction every 15 minutes, C daily call, D listings only), the call collecting now with its indicative price and volume and nominal close, the next call, the LULD band and any limit state, pause or halt.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketMm**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketmm) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/mm | Your market-maker programmes, the last 30 days of obligations, rebates earned (paid only from fees already taken), and the rules.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketMmKey**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketmmkey) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/mm/key | A key&#39;s designated market maker (by the name it chose) and how many LPs quote it.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketMmMmp**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketmmmmp) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/mm/mmp | Your market-maker protection: the fill trigger, the freeze, and whether you are frozen now.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketMode**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketmode) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/mode | Your Vault Markets mode: simple (buy, sell and what it is worth) or pro (the full terminal), whether you chose it or it was set for you (new people start in simple; people already trading start in pro), and when.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketNav**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketnav) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/nav | Mark NAV, bid NAV (the resting bids) and liquidation NAV (the bids at 20% of ADV for 10 days), holding by holding, each with its ASC 820 level, mark quality and source, and your administrator&#39;s price when uploaded.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketNavFile**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketnavfile) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/nav/file/{format} | The NAV as a csv, pdf or json file.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketOpening**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketopening) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/opening/{slug} | One opening key.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketOracle**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketoracle) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/oracle | Crossly as an oracle publisher (needs CROSSLY_ORACLE_PUBLISHER_KEY + CROSSLY_ORACLE_NETWORK): the network (pyth or switchboard), the ed25519 key every value is signed with, each published index feed with its latest attempt, and 24 h counts of published, skipped and failed. Only built-in indexes priced from A/B marks are published; Crossly never reads its own feed back.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketOracleBySlug**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketoraclebyslug) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/oracle/{slug} | The latest published oracle value for an index: level, fixed-point price and confidence at the feed exponent, confidence in bps, staleness in seconds, coverage, the exact signed message and its ed25519 signature with the signer key, and whether it is fresh.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketOrderHandling**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketorderhandling) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/order-handling | How orders are handled (the 606/607-style disclosure): one book, self-trade prevention, protected market orders, liquidations by auction, halts, dealers and fees.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketP2pDashboard**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketp2pdashboard) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/dashboard | Peer-to-peer vault loans at a glance: what you owe as a borrower and are owed as a lender (principal + interest to the second), daily interest, called loans, your open offers, the gates and the rules (auction hours, rate ceiling, size bounds, call grace).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketP2pLoan**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketp2ploan) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/loans/{id} | One P2P loan with its money log (fund, repay, refinance). A loan in a running refinancing auction can be read by any lender, without the log.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketPaper**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketpaper) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/paper | Your paper trading account (play money only, never real or convertible; opened at $10,000 the first time): play cash, buying power, paper positions at the real marks, P&amp;L, working paper orders, recent paper fills and this week on the leaderboard.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketPaperQuote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketpaperquote) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/paper/quote | What a paper order would fill against the real book now, without placing it: units fillable inside the limit (a market order uses the 5% protection band around the mark), average price, simulated fee, and the queue ahead of any rest.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketPartnerMe**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketpartnerme) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/partners/me | Your Vault Drop-off Partnership (null when you never applied): status, partner code, fee share and cap, totals and batches.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketPool**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketpool) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/pool | The CBX market staking pool: size, value, what it lends, utilisation, rate, insurance fund.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketPopReport**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketpopreport) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/pop-reports | Grading population report for one market (&#x60;bookKey&#x60;): focus (on a graded market: its grade, pop &#x3D; copies graded at exactly that grade, higher &#x3D; copies graded better by the same grader, total, asOf and source), graders (each grader ladder for the card, best grade first, from one report: the newest, the population feed over a hand entry on the same date), provider.connected, and status (ok, not_synced, no_report or no_source) with explain, which says in plain words what is missing. source is provider (the population feed) or ops (entered by Crossly from the grader published report). Counts only, never a price.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketPortfolio**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketportfolio) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/portfolio | Your vault holdings at the market price, with cost and P&amp;L, and units you have out on loan.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketPortfolioHistory**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketportfoliohistory) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/portfolio/history | Your Vault Markets value by day at the marks (vault units, units on loan, positions) and today&#39;s return against yesterday&#39;s close.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketPortfolioMargin**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketportfoliomargin) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/portfolio-margin | Portfolio margin for a firm sub-account (&#x60;subaccountId&#x60;): eligibility with every reason (ECP, KYB, minimum equity $500k or $100k with evidenced intraday monitoring), whether it is enrolled, the latest requirement, equity, excess and state (healthy, call or buy_in), the breakdown by category and item, the partner index futures counted, and the policy. Before enrolling, a live preview.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketPosition**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketposition) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/positions/{id} | One position with its loans, auctions and custodian instruction log.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketPositionExit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketpositionexit) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/positions/{id}/exits | A position&#39;s take-profit / stop-loss, if set.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketPositionRisk**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketpositionrisk) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/positions/{id}/risk | One position&#39;s call and liquidation marks.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketPress**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketpress) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/press | The press kit: each built-in index with level, quality, n, CSV link and a \&quot;cite as\&quot; line, plus the methodology links.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketPriceWidget**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketpricewidget) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/price-widgets/{id} | One of your price widgets, with its iframe and script-tag snippets and URLs.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketProDepth**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketprodepth) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/pro/depth | The L2 depth ladder: price levels and units per side, best first; asks show how many units are authenticated vault stock.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketProOrder**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketproorder) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/pro/orders/{id} | One order with its full event log.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketProSnapshot**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketprosnapshot) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/pro/snapshot | A book key&#39;s depth (20 levels a side), last 50 trades, 24 h stats, mark and any halt, in one call.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketProStat**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketprostat) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/pro/stats | 24 h open, high, low, last, volume and change on a book key.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketProStream**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketprostream) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/pro/stream | A long-lived text/event-stream for a book key: &#x60;snapshot&#x60; first, then &#x60;depth&#x60;, &#x60;trades&#x60;, &#x60;mark&#x60; and &#x60;halt&#x60; events as they change.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketProfile**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketprofile) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/profile | Your public vault profile switch: on or off, whether followers see new buys, your profile URL and follower count.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketProfileByHandle**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketprofilebyhandle) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/profiles/{handle} | Someone&#39;s public vault: each market&#39;s weight (bps of the marked value), followers, whether you follow, their recent buys (no prices).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketProofOfReserve**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketproofofreserve) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/proof-of-reserves | Proof of reserves: the latest daily Merkle root over every unit in custody (leaf count, stored / listed / grading / withdrawing, pledged, on loan, open loans, receipts, insured value), its Solana memo anchor when VAULT_POR_ANCHOR_KEY is set, the last &#x60;days&#x60; roots, and the exact hashing method.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketProofOfReserveByDay**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketproofofreservebyday) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/proof-of-reserves/{day} | One day&#39;s proof-of-reserves snapshot (root, counts, anchor).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketProofOfReserveMe**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketproofofreserveme) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/proof-of-reserves/me | Your inclusion proofs for a day (latest by default): each of your units&#39; leaf fields, salt, owner commitment, leaf hash and sibling path. Verify with your OWN user id: commitment &#x3D; SHA-256(JSON[\&quot;crossly-por-owner-v1\&quot;, userId, salt]); then the leaf; then climb the path to the published root.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketRate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketrate) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/rates | Daily borrow APR (lent units) and pool APR on a book key, with the pool as a whole, oldest first.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketReport**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketreport) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/report | The quarterly Crossly Vault Report (&#x60;quarter&#x60; like 2026-q3; default the latest): index levels, top movers, most-lent markets, grade premiums. Book data only.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketRfq**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketrfq) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/rfq/{id} | One quote request: the requester sees every answer (anonymous); a responder sees the request and their own answer.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketRiskReport**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketriskreport) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/risk-report | Historical VaR 95/99 1d/10d and Bangia LVaR (model figures: labelled, with n and a band, null below the data threshold), stress scenarios (category −30%, CBX −50%, books thin to 25%, index drawdowns), concentration and counterparty exposure.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketRiskStat**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketriskstat) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/risk-stats/{bookKey} | Risk statistics for a book key (weekly returns from A-quality marks only) or an index slug (its closes): annualised volatility raw and Geltner-desmoothed (labelled), max drawdown and beta to the Composite. Needs 26 weekly returns, else no numbers.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketSetGoal**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketsetgoal) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/set-goals/{id} | One set goal: every piece in the set order, held (with the vault unit) or missing (with the cheapest marked book key that meets the target and its mark), the conditions and grades seen, and the working basket bid with its legs.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketSetGoalBasketPlan**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketsetgoalbasketplan) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/set-goals/{id}/basket-plan | What a basket bid would place now, without placing it: one leg per missing piece (cheapest first, the mark plus maxBpsOverMark as the limit), the pieces skipped and why, and the most it can cost (limits plus the worst-case buyer fee), never more than budgetCents.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketShareCard**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketsharecard) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/share-cards/{id} | One share card as the public sees it, with its image and link paths.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketStatement**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketstatement) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/statements | The last 24 months with whether each statement is issued (frozen, with its sha256).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketStatementByMonth**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketstatementbymonth) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/statements/{month} | A monthly statement (&#x60;month&#x60; like 2026-10): units held at month end, loans, pledges, positions, fills, fees, storage, and cash quoted from the custodian and labelled as its record. Issued (frozen) once the month ends.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketStatementFile**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketstatementfile) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/statements/{month}/file/{format} | A monthly statement as a csv, pdf or json file.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketSupply**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketsupply) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/supply | A book key&#39;s units in the vault and authentications passed, by day (oldest first, today live).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketSupplyBatch**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketsupplybatch) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/supply/batches/{id} | A box sent to the vault: its prepaid label and tracking, or why it has no label.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketSupplyRoundUp**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketsupplyroundup) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/supply/round-up | Your seller round-up: the share of each payout that sets a vault buy&#39;s budget, the budget so far and what it is doing. &#x60;roundUp&#x60; is null when none is set.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketTax**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarkettax) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/tax | Your tax year on the vault markets: units bought and sold with cost, proceeds, gain and term; lending fees, staking interest (estimate), market-maker rewards; closed positions. Links to the CSV and PDF.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketTaxCsv**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarkettaxcsv) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/tax.csv | The tax year as a CSV: one row per sale, purchase, gift, income line and closed position.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketTaxLossSuggestion**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarkettaxlosssuggestion) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/tax/loss-suggestions | Tax-loss suggestions: the units you hold whose mark is below your cost, each with its lot (acquired when and how, cost in cents), short or long term if sold today and the day a short lot turns long, the estimated loss at the mark before selling fees, mark quality, what stops a sale, and a link to the ordinary sell ticket. With totals by term, the gains realised so far this year, and a not-tax-advice disclosure. Never sells.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketTaxPdf**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarkettaxpdf) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/tax.pdf | The tax year as a PDF summary.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketTca**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarkettca) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/tca | Your pro orders in a window (&#x60;from&#x60;, &#x60;to&#x60;; default 90 days) with arrival mark and mid, average fill, implementation shortfall, slippage, fill rate and time to fill.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketTcaByOrderId**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarkettcabyorderid) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/tca/{orderId} | One order&#39;s TCA, including slippage against the VWAP of eligible trades while it worked.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketTod**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarkettod) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/tod | Your transfer-on-death setup: the gate and terms, whether you accepted them, your beneficiaries and shares, units with their designations and who the plan gives them to today, and open claims on your account (which you can stop). A TodOverview object, not a collection.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketTodMyClaim**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarkettodmyclaim) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/tod/my-claims | Claims you filed as a beneficiary: status in words, documents sent (names only), the waiting period, and units moved or held. A TodMyClaims object, not a collection.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketUnitAr**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketunitar) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/units/{id}/ar | A vault unit as an AR model, for \&quot;See it in your room\&quot; (the web&#39;s /ar/vault-&lt;id&gt; page hands it to Scene Viewer, WebXR or Quick Look): title, model (a path under /models, without .glb, at real size), scanUrl (the unit&#39;s own captured mesh, when it has one), imageUrl, art, tint, scale, bookKey, backPath and mine. Your own units always; somebody else&#39;s only while they show it to visitors in a home you may enter (else 404).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketUnitEvidence**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketunitevidence) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/units/{id}/evidence | Evidence on a vault unit:its 3D scans (model, texture, listing photos) and its grade report (grader, grade, cert, verified against the grader record, hub intake photos). No owner identity.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketUnitQuote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketunitquote) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/units/{id}/quote | The market around a vault unit: its mark and source, the best bid that could take it, the Instant Exit price (the best bid) and a pack pull&#39;s own sell-back credit.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketUnitStory**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketunitstory) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/units/{id}/story | One vault unit&#39;s story, oldest first: intake (with scan and grade evidence when it has some), grades, owners, sales at their prices, loans, pledges, Hall of Grails exhibitions, receipt-token exports and redemptions, and each warehouse-receipt hash-chain step (re-verified on read: chain.ok). People are named only while their public vault profile is on; others are &#39;A collector&#39;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketValuationStatement**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketvaluationstatement) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/valuation-statements/{id} | One statement you issued: every unit, the totals by mark quality, the methodology, its sha256, its audit-chain seal and the verify link.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketValuationStatementPdf**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketvaluationstatementpdf) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/valuation-statements/{id}/pdf | One statement you issued as a PDF.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketValuationStatementPreview**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketvaluationstatementpreview) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/valuation-statements/preview | An insurance or appraisal statement for a day (purpose, asOf YYYY-MM-DD, default today), built but not issued: every unit you held that day at its mark, with quality, last trade, grade and cert, and the total. No id, no hash.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketValuationStatementVerify**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketvaluationstatementverify) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/valuation-statements/{id}/verify | Check any issued statement by its id, as an insurer would: purpose, date, totals by mark quality and the sha256; intact (the stored record still hashes to it), seal.ok (its audit-chain entry still carries it) and, with ?hash&#x3D;, whether yours matches. Never the holder or the units.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketValue**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketvalue) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/value | A catalogue item&#39;s vault book keys (&#x60;?skuId&#x3D;&#x60;, optional &#x60;variantId&#x60;): each key&#39;s display mark with quality and n, units in the vault, the Instant Exit price a holder would get now, and whether it can be shipped in (ungraded keys).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketVaultSuggestionItem**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketvaultsuggestionitem) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/vault-suggestions/items/{id} | One inventory item: its vault suggestion, or in plain words why there is none (&#x60;status&#x60;: suggested, not_found, no_stock, not_matched, in_vault or no_market).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketWidgetIndex**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketwidgetindex) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/widgets/index/{slug} | What the &lt;crossly-index&gt; widget shows: level, closes, quality, n, as-of, or &#x60;insufficient&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketWidgetMark**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketwidgetmark) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/widgets/mark | What the &lt;crossly-mark&gt; widget shows for &#x60;bookKey&#x60;: mark, quality, trades over 30 days, as-of, sparkline, or &#x60;insufficient&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketWidgetSummary**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketwidgetsummary) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/widget/summary | The home-screen widget summary: portfolio value at the mark with its move since the previous marks (cents and bps) and the share resting on reliable A/B marks, the three biggest movers among what you hold (the market movers when you hold nothing; &#x60;movers.source&#x60; says which), and one watchlist key (&#x60;?key&#x3D;&#x60; a book key on your watchlist; else your newest watch) with a 14-day sparkline. Every price is from the Crossly order book and carries its mark quality (A, B, C or none). Cached for five minutes.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketWorldFloor**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketworldfloor) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/world/floor | The Trading Floor in Crossly World: trades (the newest trades on every vault book in the last day, at most 40, newest first: bookKey, name, priceCents, priceText, side -- buy when a buyer took an ask, sell when a seller hit a bid, cross for an auction -- and ageS), tickers (the big board: the most-traded markets, each with its last trade price, the change from the last trade before today in basis points, trades today and the display mark with its quality), bell (Market Open: ringing for ten minutes after a new key opens, with the key and its market link, and what opens next) and trades24h. Every price is a trade on the Crossly order book; it never shows who traded. Nothing can be traded in the room.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketWorldLounge**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketworldlounge) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/world/lounge | The Vault Holders Lounge: the rule to join (held value at reliable marks, or a unit count), whether you may join now and what you hold toward it. Anyone may look in; only holders are seen there. No perks, nothing to trade.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketWorldPlaceVaultGate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketworldplacevaultgate) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/world/places/{place}/vault-gate | The vault-holder door on a Crossly World room or Space, by its presence slug (a room share slug, or space-&lt;slug&gt;): the rule (minValueCents held at reliable A/B marks and/or minUnits units, mode any or all, optionally one product, bullion family or category), the rule in words, and whether you may join now with what you hold toward it. gate is null when the place has no vault door. Anyone may look in; only holders join.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketWorth**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketworth) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/worth | What one book key (variantId:condition:gradeKey, &#x60;bookKey&#x60;) is worth: the mark in cents with its quality (A from trades, B from firm quotes, C indicative, none) and band, what Instant Exit would pay for one unit now from the best resting bid (yours left out), and your units: how many you hold, how many are free to sell, their value at the mark and what selling every free one now would fetch from today’s bids. Plain-words &#x60;headline&#x60; and &#x60;lines&#x60;. Read only.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultMarketWorthVault**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultmarketworthvault) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/worth/vault | What your whole vault is worth now: value at the marks split into reliable and indicative marks, cost and P&amp;L, today’s change, and an estimate of what the units free to sell would fetch from the bids resting on the book now. Read only.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultReceipt**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultreceipt) | **Get** /v1/vault/receipts/{receiptNo} | One of your receipts with its full hash chain (issued, transferred, pledged, released, surrendered), re-verified on read: &#x60;chain.ok&#x60; is false if any event was altered.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultReceiptToken**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultreceipttoken) | **Get** /v1/vault/receipt-tokens/{id} | One receipt token (yours, or on a unit you own now) with its chain log: built, minted, frozen, thawed, new holder seen, redeem built, burned, with explorer links.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**GetVaultReceiptTokenByMint**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#getvaultreceipttokenbymint) | **Get** /v1/vault/receipt-tokens/by-mint/{mint} | What a Solana mint is: the warehouse receipt number and item it stands for, and whether the token is live, frozen (unit pledged or lent) or burned, and whether the unit is still on a vault shelf. Names no person and no wallet.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketAccounts**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketaccounts) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/accounts | Every account you can act on: your own, and each sub-account your entity memberships reach, with your role.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketAdlEvents**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketadlevents) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/adl/events | The ADL events your positions were part of, newest first: role bankrupt (yours went under water) or counterparty (yours was first in line), the price both closed at and the transfer status.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketAdlPositions**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketadlpositions) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/adl/positions | Your live positions, each with its place in its item ADL queue: lights 5 (first in line) to 1 (last), 0 when not in the queue (only profitable positions are), ranked by P&amp;L% x effective leverage.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketAgentDrafts**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketagentdrafts) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/agent/drafts | Limit orders the shopping agent drafted for you, newest first. A draft places nothing: send its orderBody to POST /api/v1/vault-markets/pro/orders to place it.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketAgentWatches**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketagentwatches) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/agent/watches | Your price watches on book keys (best bid, lowest ask, last trade or market price, in cents), live ones first. &#x60;?live&#x3D;true&#x60; for live only.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketAlertLog**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketalertlog) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/alert-log | What the alerts hub did with your recent vault alerts (&#x60;?limit&#x3D;&#x60; 1 to 200, default 50): kind, title, the channels it went to, what was held (channel:reason) and the email digest state.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketAlerts**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketalerts) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/alerts | Your price alerts on book keys.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketAuctions**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketauctions) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/auctions | Open vault auction events (weekly, ending Sunday; premier, units marked $10,000+) and their lots, soonest ending first.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketBlocks**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketblocks) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/blocks | An entity&#39;s block orders, newest first, each with its plan and how it was allocated.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketBullionFamilyPegs**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketbullionfamilypegs) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/bullion/family-pegs | Your family-wide spot-pegged quotes (live, or all with status&#x3D;all): one quote resting across a bullion family books at one condition, with units left, the peg, its status and every leg (the pegged quote on each book).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketBullionKeys**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketbullionkeys) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/bullion/keys | Bullion key families (metal, form, size, mint or any mint): members, vault units, best resting bid and ask across their books, and spot as a labelled reference with what one unit of metal is worth at it. Spot is never a Crossly price.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketBullionPegs**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketbullionpegs) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/bullion/pegs | Your spot-pegged quotes (live, or all with status&#x3D;all): side, units, the peg, the order it rests as, the last price and spot it was priced from, and why it is pulled when it is.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketCollectionWorth**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketcollectionworth) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/collection-worth | Your collection valued at the display marks: rows on a vault book key with a mark are summed; rows with no mark or no key are counted, never valued.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketConfirmations**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketconfirmations) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/confirmations | Trade confirmations for your fills in a window (default 31 days): price, sale fee, maker or taker, order, vault unit and warehouse receipt.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketCreatorCohostInvites**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketcreatorcohostinvites) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/creators/cohost-invites | For creators: your invites from Crossly to co-host a Market Open launch show (invited, accepted, declined or withdrawn), each with the key, its show and the guidance for co-hosts.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketCredit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketcredit) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/credit | Your vault loans and advances (live by default): what is owed, interest accrued, loan-to-value, the liquidation line and the collateral value it is called at.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketDealerBands**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketdealerbands) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/bands | Your Instant Exit bands and whether each is being kept now.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketDealerBids**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketdealerbids) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/bids | Your standing dealer bids: open (default) or all.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketDealerPoolFills**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketdealerpoolfills) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/pools/{id}/fills | A curve pool&#39;s fills, newest first: side, price and spot after.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketDealerPools**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketdealerpools) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/pools | Your curve pools: open (default) or all, each with its curve, spot, units, buy budget, running totals, the ladder it shows and its live legs.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketDigestHistory**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketdigesthistory) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/digest/history | Past weekly digests: week, status (sent / skipped / failed), the channels used and a summary.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketEntityAudit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketentityaudit) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/audit | The entity&#39;s audit trail, newest first: hash-chained rows with actor, account, API key, IP, session and source.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketEntityConversions**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketentityconversions) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/conversions | The custodian&#39;s CBX-to-USD reward conversions for an entity that elected USD, newest first, with their status.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketEstimatesAccuracy**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketestimatesaccuracy) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/estimates-accuracy | The Crossly Estimate&#39;s track record: walk-forward backtest n, FSD and bias per method, category and liquidity bucket, and whether estimates in that cell are shown.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketFeed**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketfeed) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/feed | New vault buys by the people you follow who share them, newest first (no prices). Page with &#x60;before&#x60; (the last item&#39;s &#x60;at&#x60;).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketFollowCopyWatch**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketfollowcopywatch) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/follows/copy-watch | The collectors you copy-watch and whether each still shares bids and asks.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketFollowCopyWatchAlerts**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketfollowcopywatchalerts) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/follows/copy-watch/alerts | Your copy-watch alerts, newest first: who, bid or ask, quantity, limit price, market, and whether it still rests. Page with &#x60;before&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketFollowFeed**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketfollowfeed) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/follows/feed | Public vault activity of the collectors you follow (buys, units sent in, sales, Hall loans), newest first, never a price. Filter with &#x60;kinds&#x60;; page with &#x60;before&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketFollowFollowing**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketfollowfollowing) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/follows/following | The collectors you follow who have a public vault: what each shares and whether you copy-watch them.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketFollowLeaderboards**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketfollowleaderboards) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/follows/leaderboards | An opt-in return leaderboard (&#x60;period&#x60; 30d, 90d or 365d; &#x60;category&#x60; all or a category slug), ranked by risk-adjusted return from the book A/B marks. Percent and risk only: no dollars, no volume.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketFollowProfileActivity**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketfollowprofileactivity) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/follows/profiles/{handle}/activity | One collector public vault activity, newest first, never a price. 404 when their vault is not public.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketGifts**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketgifts) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/gifts | Gifts you sent and received, newest first, with their status.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketGradeToVault**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketgradetovault) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/grade-to-vault | Your Grade to Vault flows, newest first, each brought up to date: its step, the grading submission, the unit, the grade, the market for it, and the ask once listed. &#x60;live&#x3D;1&#x60; for those still going.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketGrading**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketgrading) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/grading | Your vault units sent to grading: where each card is in its run, and the grade once it is back.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketHallLenders**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarkethalllenders) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/hall/lenders | The vault units lent to this week&#39;s Hall of Grails exhibition and on show now, in plinth order: the item (skuId, name, imageUrl, grade), the lender as the plate names them (a display name, or \&quot;a collector\&quot;), the plate lines and how many exhibitions the unit has stood in. meta.exhibition is the week (key, title, endsAt). Names no ids and no amounts.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketIndexCommitteeMinutes**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketindexcommitteeminutes) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/index-committee/minutes | Published Index Committee minutes, newest first: attendees (names and roles), quorum, agenda, minutes and decisions.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketIndexComplaints**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketindexcomplaints) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/index-complaints | Your complaints and their answers, newest first.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketIndexConstituents**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketindexconstituents) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/indexes/{slug}/constituents | The book keys in an index with their weight, move since the last close and contribution to the index&#39;s move (basis points).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketIndexConsultations**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketindexconsultations) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/index-consultations | Consultations on index method changes (open for at least 30 days), newest first. Filter by &#x60;status&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketIndexEvents**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketindexevents) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/index-events | Every index event, newest first: add, delete, rebalance, method, correction, halt; each with when it was announced, when it takes effect, the detail and the reason. Filter by &#x60;kind&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketIndexEventsBySlug**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketindexeventsbyslug) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/indexes/{slug}/events | One index&#39;s events, newest first.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketIndexJudgments**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketindexjudgments) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/index-judgments | The expert-judgment log: every Index Committee action (halt, resume, exclude a key, void a trade, restate, retire) with its written reason.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketIndexLevelSeries**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketindexlevelseries) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/indexes/{slug}/level-series | An index&#39;s published levels. &#x60;engine&#x3D;chain&#x60; (default): daily closes. &#x60;engine&#x3D;tpd|repeat_sales&#x60;: weekly (else monthly) model estimates with a 95% band and n; labelled, never a price. &#x60;series&#x3D;settlement&#x60; returns each value as first published (never restated). Below the data gate &#x60;data&#x60; is empty and &#x60;notice&#x60; explains.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketIndexLicenceMine**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketindexlicencemine) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/index-licence/mine | Your index feed licences (the indexes each covers, or all), as ops recorded them. No licence: the feed answers 403 not_a_licensee.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketIndexMembers**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketindexmembers) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/indexes/{slug}/members | The members of an index on a day (&#x60;asOf&#x60;, default today) with their weights, shares, moves and contributions; for a past day, the inputs stored with that day&#39;s close and each member&#39;s effective dates.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketIndexMovers**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketindexmovers) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/indexes/movers | The built-in indexes that moved most since their last daily close.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketIndexes**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketindexes) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/indexes | Indexes over book keys: per category, per top brand, the Crossly 50 and your own. Each with its level now (base 1000 at inception) and change since the last daily close.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketInstantExitHistory**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketinstantexithistory) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/instant-exit/history | Your Instant Exits, newest first: quoted price, mark, and whether it filled.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketInstantExitHoldings**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketinstantexitholdings) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/instant-exit/holdings | Instant Exit quotes for everything you hold in the vault, one per book key, with units held and which are free to sell.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketJointTrades**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketjointtrades) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/trades | The joint vault trades, newest first: side, book key, limit, who started it, whose account the order sits on, and the order standing on the book.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketLargeTraderReports**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketlargetraderreports) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/large-trader-reports | Your large-trader reports (with any linked accounts, as one party): due first, then filed, then cleared in the last 90 days. Each has &#x60;action&#x60;: file | amend | refile | none.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketLendingStatements**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketlendingstatements) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/lending/statements | The days you have stored collateral statements for, newest first, with their summary.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketLoans**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketloans) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/loans | Units you lent: rate, fees earned, recall due.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketLocateMine**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketlocatemine) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/locates/mine | Your locates, newest first (held / used / released / expired).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketMidpoint**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketmidpoint) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/midpoint | Your midpoint block orders.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketMovers**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketmovers) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/movers | Vault items with the biggest 24 h moves in their market price (from the order book), with units in the vault.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketNavHistory**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketnavhistory) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/nav/history | Daily NAV snapshots (mark, bid, liquidation, Level 2 / Level 3).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketOffers**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketoffers) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/offers | The offers inbox: for each book key you hold, the best bid from someone else, units at it, the top bid levels, the mark, and your alert.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketOpening**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketopening) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/opening | Keys opening soon on the vault book (and just opened): units on the shelf, dealers quoting, the waitlist, the mark when the book has one, and your place.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketOracleHistory**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketoraclehistory) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/oracle/{slug}/history | Recent publish attempts for an index over 30 days, newest first: published, skipped (with the reason: too few items priced, stale marks, confidence too wide...) or failed (with the error). Filter with &#x60;status&#x60;, cap with &#x60;limit&#x60; (default 100).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketP2pAuctions**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketp2pauctions) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/auctions | Running refinancing auctions, soonest to end first: lenders calls (&#x60;auction&#x60;) and borrowers looking for a cheaper lender (&#x60;refiAuction&#x60;). The payoff now, the rate a new lender gets now, when it ends, and the item.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketP2pLoans**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketp2ploans) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/loans | Your P2P loans as borrower and/or lender (&#x60;role&#x60;), live by default (&#x60;status&#x60;): what is owed now, daily interest, the refinancing auction when called, and the item mark for information only (it never triggers anything).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketP2pOfferMine**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketp2poffermine) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/offers/mine | Your P2P loan offers, newest first, with how many loans each has funded.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketP2pOffers**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketp2poffers) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/offers | The open P2P loan offers book, lowest rate first: cents offered per loan, APR (bps), loans left, and the item (book key) or unit each lends against. &#x60;bookKey&#x60; narrows to one item. Lender identities are never shown.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketP2pUnits**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketp2punits) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/units | Your vault units for P2P borrowing: whether each can be pledged now (and why not), the best offers covering it, and its loan when it has one.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketPaperFills**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketpaperfills) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/paper/fills | Your paper fills since your last reset, newest first: price, simulated fee, taker or maker, and whether a visible real order (book) or a real trade (tape) filled it. Play money only.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketPaperLeaderboard**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketpaperleaderboard) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/paper/leaderboard | The weekly paper trading leaderboard (opt-in, display names only): best return on play equity this week (or a past &#x60;week&#x60;, its Monday as YYYY-MM-DD), with your own line. Nothing is won or paid.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketPaperMissions**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketpapermissions) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/paper/missions | Your paper-trading missions (play money only): each mission with what to do, your count toward its target, whether it is done and when, plus a suggested market for the \&quot;Read the mark\&quot; mission. Progress is observed by the server from your paper orders and fills, price watches, set goals and graded mark checks. Missions earn world quest progress and cosmetic pins, never money.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketPaperOrders**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketpaperorders) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/paper/orders | Your paper orders since your last reset, newest first: working, done (filled, cancelled, expired) or all. Play money only.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketPartnerAccruals**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketpartneraccruals) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/partners/accruals | Your partner fee shares, newest first: accrued on a unit&#39;s first sale, paid in Bucks once the sale is final, void if it was refunded.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketPoolStakes**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketpoolstakes) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/pool/stakes | Your stakes in the market pool and what each redeems for now.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketPortfolioMarginHistory**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketportfoliomarginhistory) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/portfolio-margin/history | The 5-minute portfolio margin recalculations for a firm sub-account over the last &#x60;hours&#x60; (default 24, at most 720), newest first: equity, requirement, excess, scenario and floor figures, gross and state.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketPositionUnits**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketpositionunits) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/positions/{id}/units | The vault units of one of your positions that you hold (a leveraged long&#39;s collateral), each with its story link.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketPositions**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketpositions) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/positions | Your positions, live (default) or closed.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketPositionsRisk**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketpositionsrisk) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/positions-risk | For each live position: its tier, health, margin state, the mark at which it is called and the mark at which it is liquidated, and the distance to it.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketPriceWidgets**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketpricewidgets) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/price-widgets | Your price widgets, newest first, each with its iframe and script-tag snippets. &#x60;meta.codes&#x60; lists your affiliate codes a widget can link through; &#x60;meta.max&#x60; is the cap on live widgets.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketProCandles**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketprocandles) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/pro/candles | OHLCV candles (1m, 5m, 15m, 1h, 4h, 1d) from the trades on a book key.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketProEvents**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketproevents) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/pro/events | Your order event log after a sequence number (new, leg, fill, replaced, cancelled, expired, done): a replayable drop copy.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketProLinks**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketprolinks) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/pro/links | Your linked orders (brackets, OCO pairs, trailing stops, position exits): live (default) or done.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketProOrders**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketproorders) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/pro/orders | Your orders: open (default) or done, optionally on one book key, or only those booked to one sub-account (&#x60;subaccountId&#x60;).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketProQuotes**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketproquotes) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/pro/quotes | Your live quotes.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketProRewards**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketprorewards) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/pro/rewards | Your daily market-maker liquidity rewards (paid in CBX).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketProTape**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketprotape) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/pro/tape | The latest trades on a book key, newest first, with which side made and which took.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketProofOfReserveLeaves**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketproofofreserveleaves) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/proof-of-reserves/{day}/leaves | One day&#39;s leaf hashes in tree order, a page at a time: enough to recount the units and rebuild the root yourself. Leaves are salted, so they name no owner.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketRecurring**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketrecurring) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/recurring | Your recurring buys: key, schedule, price cap, payment, auto-lend, next run.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketRecurringRuns**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketrecurringruns) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/recurring/{id}/runs | One recurring buy&#39;s runs: filled, skipped (with the reason, e.g. the price was over the cap) or failed.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketRecurringRuns2**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketrecurringruns2) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/recurring-runs | Every run of all your recurring buys, newest first.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketRfq**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketrfq) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/rfq | Your quote requests, newest first.
