*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketRfqInbox**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketrfqinbox) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/rfq/inbox | Open quote requests you were sent (as a dealer, DMM, LP or holder), with your live answer.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketRfqOpen**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketrfqopen) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/rfq/open | Open quote requests on one key (all-to-all: anyone with ID may answer). Anonymous.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketSetGoalBaskets**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketsetgoalbaskets) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/set-goals/{id}/baskets | A set goal basket bids, newest first, each with its budget, what it committed and spent, and every leg (piece, book key, limit, pro order id, status).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketSetGoalSets**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketsetgoalsets) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/set-goals/sets | Sets to start a goal on. With q (2+ characters): a word-start search of every catalogue set. Without: the sets you hold pieces of in the vault, then the collections you are building. Each with your live goal on it, if any.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketSetGoals**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketsetgoals) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/set-goals | Your set goals: the set, the target a piece must meet, pieces held of the total (from your vault), and the working basket bid.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketShareCards**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketsharecards) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/share-cards | Your share cards (not taken down), each as the public sees it.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketSupplyDeposits**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketsupplydeposits) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/supply/deposits | Your units sent to the vault, newest first, each with its state and its key&#39;s Instant Exit line (\&quot;Instant Exit now: $X (N% of mark)\&quot; or \&quot;No market yet\&quot;). Filter with &#x60;inventoryItemIds&#x60; (comma-separated).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketSupplyInvites**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketsupplyinvites) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/supply/invites | Market Open invites to you as a dealer: keys Crossly is opening and asks you to quote.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketValuationStatements**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketvaluationstatements) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/valuation-statements | The insurance and appraisal statements you issued, newest first: purpose, date, units, total at the marks, hash and verify link.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketVaultSuggestions**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketvaultsuggestions) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/vault-suggestions | What to send to the vault: your inventory items whose book key has a liquid vault market (tier A, B or C), best first. Each has a score (0 to 100) and its reasons, the market (tier, mark and its quality, best bid, best vault ask, spread), Instant Exit now, your list price, days listed without selling, and the vault fee. &#x60;meta.summary&#x60; counts what was left out and why. Inform only; send with POST /api/v1/vault-markets/supply/send.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketWatchlist**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketwatchlist) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/watchlist | Your watched book keys with mark, 24 h and 7 d change, and the last 30 daily marks (sparkline).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketWidgetTokens**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketwidgettokens) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/widget/tokens | Your live home-screen widget tokens, one per phone: id, name (the phone), public prefix, created and last used. The secret is never returned again.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketWorldCohosts**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketworldcohosts) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/world/cohosts | Market Open launch shows a verified creator co-hosts that are on now or opening soon: the key, its phase, the show, the creator (named only while their vault profile is public), their room link and, when the creator is in the creator programme, the disclosure that they may earn a share of the fee Crossly keeps. Never a prompt to trade.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketWorldCreatorRooms**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketworldcreatorrooms) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/world/creator-rooms | Verified creators whose public vault shelf is a world room: title, the creator, the room link and the disclosure. Rooms show each market share of the shelf, never amounts.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultMarketWorldMarketOpen**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultmarketworldmarketopen) | **Get** /v1/vault-markets/world/market-open | Market Open events in the Crossly World Commons: keys opening soon and those that opened in the last few hours, live first then soonest, each with its board line, units on the shelf, dealers quoting and the launch show when there is one. Never a prompt to trade.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultReceiptTokenWallets**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultreceipttokenwallets) | **Get** /v1/vault/receipt-tokens/wallets | Your verified Solana wallets and whether each is on the receipt-token allow-list (receipt tokens move only between allow-listed wallets). meta.state is the gate.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultReceiptTokens**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultreceipttokens) | **Get** /v1/vault/receipt-tokens | Your vault units with their warehouse receipt and receipt-token state (not exported, waiting for a signature, in a wallet, frozen, redeemed), whether each can be exported now and why not. meta.state is the gate (open, missing envs, program id, authority, terms version, cluster); meta.history is your last 50 exports.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultReceipts**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultreceipts) | **Get** /v1/vault/receipts | Your warehouse receipts (documents of title for your vault units), live first. meta.receipts says whether receipts are registered (VAULT_WAREHOUSE_ISSUER) and their terms version.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**ListVaultUnitList**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#listvaultunitlist) | **Get** /v1/vault/unit-list | The published unit list (bar-list equivalent): every unit on a vault shelf with its warehouse receipt number (when receipts are registered), book key, shelf location code, insured value, status, pledged / on-loan flags and a per-receipt hashed holder (never a name or id). Filter with &#x60;bookKey&#x60;; pages with &#x60;page&#x60; / &#x60;limit&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketAlertPref**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketalertpref) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/alert-prefs | Change vault alert settings: &#x60;matrix&#x60; ({ kind: { channel: true|false } }, merged onto what is set), &#x60;quietHours&#x60; ({ start: \&quot;HH:MM\&quot;, end: \&quot;HH:MM\&quot;, tz } or null to clear) and &#x60;telegram&#x60; (the Telegram master switch). Locked switches are ignored. Returns the new settings.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketBlockAllocation**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketblockallocation) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/blocks/{orderId}/allocation | Post-trade allocation: set or replace the plan for a buy you placed for one of the entity&#39;s sub-accounts (that sub-account leads). Refused once any share has moved.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketChannel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketchannel) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/channels | Change the switches: &#x60;telegramAlerts&#x60;, &#x60;telegramDigest&#x60;, &#x60;pushCapPerDay&#x60; (0 to 20). Margin calls and recalls are never capped. Returns the new view.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketDealerBand**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketdealerband) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/bands | Commit to an Instant Exit band on a key: a standing bid for &#x60;units&#x60; kept at &#x60;bandBps&#x60; of the mark, never above &#x60;maxPriceCents&#x60;, re-quoted as the mark moves.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketDealerPool**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketdealerpool) | **Patch** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/pools/{id} | Change a curve pool: spot, step, fee, levels, units, buy budget, floor, ceiling, or pause / resume. Re-quoted at once.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketEntityMember**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketentitymember) | **Patch** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/members/{memberId} | Admin: change a member&#39;s role, limits or sub-accounts. The last admin cannot be demoted.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketEntitySubaccountLimit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketentitysubaccountlimit) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/subaccounts/{subId}/limits | Admin: set a sub-account&#39;s limits (per-order notional, units per key, gross, net, leverage), held to the entity&#39;s caps. &#x60;limits: null&#x60; clears them.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketEntityUsdElection**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketentityusdelection) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/usd-election | Admin: elect USD for the entity (&#x60;usdOnly&#x60;). Its sub-accounts then post collateral in USD only and pay every fee in USD, and any CBX reward paid to one of them is converted to USD by the custodian in that account. Audited; it can be turned off again.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketFollowCopyWatch**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketfollowcopywatch) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/follows/copy-watch/{handle} | Copy-watch a collector who shares bids and asks: you get an alert when they rest a new one. It never places an order for you.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketFollowSharing**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketfollowsharing) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/follows/sharing | Turn sharing switches on or off (&#x60;isPublic&#x60;, &#x60;showBuys&#x60;, &#x60;deposits&#x60;, &#x60;sales&#x60;, &#x60;hallLoans&#x60;, &#x60;orders&#x60;, &#x60;leaderboard&#x60;). A switch shows only what happens after it goes on; all but the profile itself need the profile public.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketHomeUnit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarkethomeunit) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/home/units | Show exactly these vault units in your Crossly World home: { units: [{ vaultItemId, visitors }] }. Units left out come out of their cases; units already standing stay where they are; new ones go into a free display-case or shelf slot, else a glass case of their own along a wall. visitors: true lets people who may enter your home see that unit (default false: only you). Returns placed, unplaced (no free case: the home is full), refused (with why) and the desk. Display only: nothing moves.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketIndex**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketindex) | **Patch** /v1/vault-markets/indexes/{slug} | Rename, re-weight, share or change the keys of an index of your own; its level carries on.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketLendingElection**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketlendingelection) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/lending/election | Change your lender election (applies to loans opened after the change). Needs lending.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketMmMmp**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketmmmmp) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/mm/mmp | Set market-maker protection: N units or $X filled on your quotes within T seconds pulls every quote and freezes quoting for a set time.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketMode**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketmode) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/mode | Choose your Vault Markets mode: &#x60;mode&#x60; simple or pro. Remembered for you on the web and in the app. A display preference only: it places nothing and moves no money.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketOfferAlert**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketofferalert) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/offers/alert | Set (or clear with null) the bid price that should always notify you on a key you hold, and mute or unmute offer notices on it.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketPaperLeaderboard**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketpaperleaderboard) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/paper/leaderboard | Join or leave the weekly paper leaderboard. Joining needs a display name (2 to 24 letters, numbers, spaces, dots, dashes or underscores), never your real name. Returns &#x60;{ optIn, displayName }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketPositionExit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketpositionexit) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/positions/{id}/exits | Set a position&#39;s take-profit / stop-loss: a long is closed, a short covered, when the last trade reaches one. Both null clears them.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketPriceWidget**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketpricewidget) | **Patch** /v1/vault-markets/price-widgets/{id} | Change a widget: name, range, layout, theme, affiliate code (null for none) or allowed sites. The market or index it shows never changes: make a new widget for another.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketProfile**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketprofile) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/profile | Turn your public vault profile on or off (&#x60;isPublic&#x60;) and whether followers see your new buys (&#x60;showBuys&#x60;). It shows weights by market, never dollars.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketRecurring**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketrecurring) | **Patch** /v1/vault-markets/recurring/{id} | Change a recurring buy&#39;s schedule, caps, cards or auto-lend, or pause / resume it.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketSetGoal**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketsetgoal) | **Patch** /v1/vault-markets/set-goals/{id} | Change what a piece must be to count (condition, grade mode, grade). Refused while a basket bid works: cancel it first.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketShareCard**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketsharecard) | **Patch** /v1/vault-markets/share-cards/{id} | Show or hide the dollar amounts on one of your share cards (&#x60;showAmounts&#x60;).
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketSupplyDeposit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketsupplydeposit) | **Patch** /v1/vault-markets/supply/deposits/{id} | Change a deposit&#39;s toggles: &#x60;autoListBps&#x60; (list at that share of the mark once stored; null &#x3D; do not) and &#x60;lendWhenIdle&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketSupplyRoundUp**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketsupplyroundup) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/supply/round-up | Set the round-up: &#x60;shareBps&#x60; (1%-50%) of each payout released to you adds to a budget; when it covers the cheapest vault unit on &#x60;bookKey&#x60;, one unit is bought with your saved card, never above the budget.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketTodBeneficiary**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarkettodbeneficiary) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/tod/beneficiaries | Replace your beneficiary list (keep an id to edit one): full name, contact (email or @username), relationship, and share in basis points; shares add up to 10000. Returns &#x60;{ beneficiaries, removed }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketTodDesignation**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarkettoddesignation) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/tod/designations | Leave one of your units to one beneficiary (beneficiaryId null clears it; the unit then follows the shares). Returns &#x60;{ vaultItemId, beneficiaryId }&#x60;.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketWorldRoomVaultGate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketworldroomvaultgate) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/world/rooms/{id}/vault-gate | Set the vault-holder door on your own room (its collection rooms follow): visitors must hold minValueCents (cents, at reliable A/B marks) and/or minUnits units in the Crossly vault, mode any (either) or all (both), optionally only one product (scope kind product, ref the catalogue product id), bullion family (bullion, the family slug) or category (category, the slug). Send null to open it again. Returns &#x60;{ gate }&#x60;. Moves no money.
*VaultMarketsApi* | [**UpdateVaultMarketWorldSpaceVaultGate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#updatevaultmarketworldspacevaultgate) | **Put** /v1/vault-markets/world/spaces/{slug}/vault-gate | Set the vault-holder door on a Space you host: the same rule as a room (minValueCents at reliable marks and/or minUnits, mode, optional scope). Send null to open it again. Returns &#x60;{ gate }&#x60;. Moves no money.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3Alert**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3alert) | **Post** /v1/web3/alerts | Set a price alert on a token, or on a whole collection without tokenId: you are notified when one is listed at or below &#x60;price&#x60; (the chain&#39;s native coin). Setting it again moves the price.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3Auction**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3auction) | **Post** /v1/web3/auctions | Start an English auction on a token in your linked wallet: &#x60;reserve&#x60; is the opening bid in the chain&#39;s native coin, &#x60;hours&#x60; its length (1–720). Bids must rise 5%; a late bid extends the end by 10 minutes.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3AuctionCancel**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3auctioncancel) | **Post** /v1/web3/auctions/{id}/cancel | Call off your auction while it has no bids.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3CreatorClaim**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3creatorclaim) | **Post** /v1/web3/creator/claims | Claim an EVM collection as its creator: its owner() must be one of your verified wallets. Returns &#x60;{ chain, contract }&#x60;.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3Crosslist**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3crosslist) | **Post** /v1/web3/crosslist | Submit signed Seaport listings for several marketplaces at once (crossly, opensea); results per marketplace.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3DropRemind**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3dropremind) | **Post** /v1/web3/drops/{id}/remind | Turn the 15-minute reminder for a drop on, or off if it already is. Returns &#x60;{ remindMe }&#x60;.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3Favorite**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3favorite) | **Post** /v1/web3/favorites | Favourite an NFT, or unfavourite it if it already is. Returns &#x60;{ favorited }&#x60;.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3Launch**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3launch) | **Post** /v1/web3/launches | Draft a launchpad collection: name, symbol, supply, mint price (wei of the chain’s native currency), schedule, optional holder phase and royalty. Crossly takes 5% of each mint inside the contract.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3LaunchAirdrop**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3launchairdrop) | **Post** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/airdrop | Plan an airdrop (one free NFT each, up to 500) to &#x60;addresses&#x60; and/or the holders of &#x60;holdersOf&#x60;. Returns the transactions for the owner wallet; no payment moves.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3LaunchArt**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3launchart) | **Post** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/art | Append per-token art (image URL, name, traits), up to the supply, before the reveal. Returns &#x60;{ count }&#x60;.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3LaunchConfirmDeploy**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3launchconfirmdeploy) | **Post** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/confirm-deploy | Confirm a sent deploy transaction: the contract must be the Crossly launch contract with this draft’s settings; it then goes live.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3LaunchFollowCreator**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3launchfollowcreator) | **Post** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/follow-creator | Follow the creator of a launch: their launches are announced to you, and again when each opens. Returns &#x60;{ following }&#x60;.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3LaunchReveal**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3launchreveal) | **Post** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/reveal | Reveal the art: one random offset maps every token to its asset for good. Returns &#x60;{ revealedAt }&#x60;.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3Order**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3order) | **Post** /v1/web3/orders | Place a signed Seaport 1.6 order (listing, offer or collection offer) in the Crossly book.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3OrderCancel**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3ordercancel) | **Post** /v1/web3/orders/{chain}/{hash}/cancel | Take one of your orders out of the Crossly book.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3OrderFulfillmentBatch**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3orderfulfillmentbatch) | **Post** /v1/web3/orders/fulfillment-batch | Fulfilments for sweeping several listings in one Seaport transaction; unfillable ones are skipped with a reason.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3Phygital**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3phygital) | **Post** /v1/web3/phygitals | Make an NFT you hold redeemable for one of your physical inventory items.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3PhygitalRedeem**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3phygitalredeem) | **Post** /v1/web3/phygitals/{id}/redeem | Redeem a phygital you hold (checked on-chain) for its physical item, with a shipping address. Once per phygital.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3PhygitalRedemptionShip**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3phygitalredemptionship) | **Post** /v1/web3/phygitals/redemptions/{id}/ship | Mark a redemption shipped with its carrier and tracking number; the redeemer is notified.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3RaribleDraft**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3raribledraft) | **Post** /v1/web3/rarible/draft | Draft a Rarible V2 listing for an NFT in your linked wallet: the EIP-712 order to sign and whether Rarible&#39;s transfer proxy is approved.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3RaribleListing**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3rariblelisting) | **Post** /v1/web3/rarible/listings | Submit a signed Rarible V2 listing: the signature is verified, then the order is posted to Rarible and recorded.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3SolanaListTx**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3solanalisttx) | **Post** /v1/web3/solana/{marketplace}/list-tx | Build an unsigned listing transaction on the Crossly book, Tensor or Magic Eden for a Solana NFT in your linked wallet.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3SolanaListing**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3solanalisting) | **Post** /v1/web3/solana/{marketplace}/listings | Record a Crossly-book, Tensor or Magic Eden listing your wallet sent (read back from the chain first).
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3SolanaOffer**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3solanaoffer) | **Post** /v1/web3/solana/offers | Record a sent bid; it is recorded only once its trade state exists on chain.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3SolanaOfferAcceptTx**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3solanaofferaccepttx) | **Post** /v1/web3/solana/offers/{hash}/accept-tx | Build the sale into a bid from your linked wallet that holds the NFT (sell + execute_sale, one transaction).
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3SolanaOfferCancelTx**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3solanaoffercanceltx) | **Post** /v1/web3/solana/offers/{hash}/cancel-tx | Build the cancel of your bid: the SOL your other bids do not need comes back.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3SolanaOfferConfirm**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3solanaofferconfirm) | **Post** /v1/web3/solana/offers/{hash}/confirm | Apply a sent accept or cancel once the chain shows the bid closed.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3SolanaOfferTx**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3solanaoffertx) | **Post** /v1/web3/solana/offers/tx | Build a bid on a Solana NFT on Crossly&#39;s book for your linked wallet to sign; the SOL goes into your Auction House escrow.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3SolanaOrderBuyTx**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3solanaorderbuytx) | **Post** /v1/web3/solana/orders/{hash}/buy-tx | Build the unsigned transaction that buys a Solana listing (price capped at the listed price).
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3SolanaOrderConfirm**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3solanaorderconfirm) | **Post** /v1/web3/solana/orders/{hash}/confirm | Apply a sent Solana buy or delist to the book (the transaction is read back from the chain).
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3SolanaOrderDelistTx**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3solanaorderdelisttx) | **Post** /v1/web3/solana/orders/{hash}/delist-tx | Build the unsigned transaction that takes your Solana listing down.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3SolanaTensorBidAcceptTx**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3solanatensorbidaccepttx) | **Post** /v1/web3/solana/tensor-bids/accept-tx | Build the transaction that sells your NFT into a Tensor bid, never below &#x60;minLamports&#x60;.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3SolanaTensorBidCancelTx**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3solanatensorbidcanceltx) | **Post** /v1/web3/solana/tensor-bids/cancel-tx | Build the transaction that cancels your Tensor bid.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3SolanaTensorBidPlaceTx**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3solanatensorbidplacetx) | **Post** /v1/web3/solana/tensor-bids/place-tx | Build a Tensor collection bid (or trait bid with &#x60;trait&#x60;) for your Solana wallet to sign. Price in lamports.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3TokenRefresh**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3tokenrefresh) | **Post** /v1/web3/tokens/{chain}/{contract}/{tokenId}/refresh | Re-read a token&#39;s metadata from the chain&#39;s provider (name, traits, image — a new image is copied to Crossly&#39;s CDN). Once per token per 10 minutes.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3Transaction**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3transaction) | **Post** /v1/web3/transactions | Apply a Seaport transaction&#39;s fills and cancels to the Crossly book (read from the receipt).
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3WalletChallenge**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3walletchallenge) | **Post** /v1/web3/wallets/challenge | Start linking a wallet: returns the message the wallet must sign.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3WalletSync**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3walletsync) | **Post** /v1/web3/wallets/{id}/sync | Import a linked wallet&#39;s NFTs into inventory; archives ones that have left the wallet.
*Web3Api* | [**CreateWeb3WalletVerify**](docs/Web3Api.md#createweb3walletverify) | **Post** /v1/web3/wallets/verify | Finish linking a wallet with its signature over the challenge message.
*Web3Api* | [**DeleteWeb3Alert**](docs/Web3Api.md#deleteweb3alert) | **Delete** /v1/web3/alerts/{id} | Delete a price alert.
*Web3Api* | [**DeleteWeb3HolderPerk**](docs/Web3Api.md#deleteweb3holderperk) | **Delete** /v1/web3/holder-perks/{id} | Remove a holder perk. Returns &#x60;{ ok }&#x60;.
*Web3Api* | [**DeleteWeb3Launch**](docs/Web3Api.md#deleteweb3launch) | **Delete** /v1/web3/launches/{id} | Delete a launch draft that never went live. Returns &#x60;{ ok }&#x60;.
*Web3Api* | [**DeleteWeb3LaunchFollowCreator**](docs/Web3Api.md#deleteweb3launchfollowcreator) | **Delete** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/follow-creator | Stop following the creator of a launch. Returns &#x60;{ following }&#x60;.
*Web3Api* | [**DeleteWeb3MarketplaceKey**](docs/Web3Api.md#deleteweb3marketplacekey) | **Delete** /v1/web3/marketplace-keys/{marketplace} | Remove your own key for a marketplace; Crossly&#39;s key is used again.
*Web3Api* | [**DeleteWeb3Wallet**](docs/Web3Api.md#deleteweb3wallet) | **Delete** /v1/web3/wallets/{id} | Unlink a wallet. Its NFTs stay in inventory but stop syncing.
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3Auction**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3auction) | **Get** /v1/web3/auctions/{chain}/{contract}/{tokenId} | A token&#39;s live (or latest) auction: reserve, end, top bid, bid count and the minimum next bid.
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3Browse**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3browse) | **Get** /v1/web3/browse | The Web3 directory&#39;s categories and chains, with how many collections each holds.
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3Collection**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3collection) | **Get** /v1/web3/collections/{chain}/{contract} | A collection: metadata and Crossly stats (floor, listed, volume, best collection offer).
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3CollectionDepth**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3collectiondepth) | **Get** /v1/web3/collections/{chain}/{contract}/depth | A collection&#39;s Crossly order book by price level — asks cheapest first, bids dearest first, with running totals — and the floor of every trait value.
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3CollectionInsight**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3collectioninsight) | **Get** /v1/web3/collections/{chain}/{contract}/insights | On-chain insights for a collection: holders, trait counts across the whole collection, recent transfers and a 30-day activity series.
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3CollectionItem**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3collectionitem) | **Get** /v1/web3/collections/{chain}/{contract}/items | A collection&#39;s NFTs: filter by traits (repeat trait&#x3D;Type:Value; OR within a type, AND across), search by name or token id, sort by id, rarity or Crossly price. Whole-collection once indexed.
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3CollectionTrait**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3collectiontrait) | **Get** /v1/web3/collections/{chain}/{contract}/traits | Every trait type and value in a collection, with how many tokens have each.
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3CollectionTraitToken**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3collectiontraittoken) | **Get** /v1/web3/collections/{chain}/{contract}/trait-tokens | Token ids in a collection with one trait — what a trait offer commits to.
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3Collector**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3collector) | **Get** /v1/web3/collector | Your collector perks: how many different NFTs you bought through Crossly, your Collector pin tier, relist rewards paid, and each NFT you still hold with the Bought on Crossly mark.
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3Creator**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3creator) | **Get** /v1/web3/creator/{chain}/{contract} | A collection’s creator dashboard: sales by marketplace and day, estimated royalties, holders, and for a launch the mint revenue and what is waiting to be withdrawn (wei of the chain’s native currency).
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3CrosslistQuote**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3crosslistquote) | **Get** /v1/web3/crosslist/quote | Each marketplace&#39;s terms for listing a token: Crossly fee, OpenSea required fees and zone, Tensor availability.
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3Featured**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3featured) | **Get** /v1/web3/featured | The NFT home&#39;s featured collections: chosen by Crossly, else the hottest.
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3Inventory**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3inventory) | **Get** /v1/web3/inventory/{itemId} | The NFT behind an inventory item: its token, wallet, and open listings on every marketplace (null if it is not an NFT).
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3LaunchAllowlistRootTx**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3launchallowlistroottx) | **Get** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/allowlist/root-tx | The setAllowlistRoot transaction for the allowlist Crossly holds (EVM, live): &#x60;{ chain, from, to, data }&#x60;.
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3LaunchDeployTx**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3launchdeploytx) | **Get** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/deploy-tx | The contract-creation transaction for the draft: send it from the owner wallet, then confirm it. Returns &#x60;{ chain, from, data }&#x60;.
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3LaunchTool**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3launchtool) | **Get** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/tools | A launch’s creator tools: allowlist size and root (and whether the contract has it yet), uploaded art and reveal, supply minted.
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3OrderFulfillment**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3orderfulfillment) | **Get** /v1/web3/orders/{chain}/{hash}/fulfillment | The signed order a taker passes to Seaport, after re-checking it can still fill.
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3Phygital**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3phygital) | **Get** /v1/web3/phygitals/{chain}/{contract}/{tokenId} | A token&#39;s phygital: the physical item it can be redeemed for, and whether it has been.
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3Phygital2**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3phygital2) | **Get** /v1/web3/phygitals | Phygitals you linked (with each redemption and its shipping address) and ones you redeemed.
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3Portfolio**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3portfolio) | **Get** /v1/web3/portfolio | Your NFTs with value (Crossly floor, else recent sales) and cost (what a linked wallet paid), realised sales, and USD totals at each day’s coin price.
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3PortfolioTax**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3portfoliotax) | **Get** /v1/web3/portfolio/tax | One calendar year of NFT disposals from your linked wallets as CSV text: acquired, sold, proceeds, cost basis, gain (coin and USD).
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3Profile**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3profile) | **Get** /v1/web3/profiles/{address} | A wallet&#39;s public profile: NFTs held, open Crossly listings, and activity.
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3Resolve**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3resolve) | **Get** /v1/web3/resolve/{chain}/{slug} | A collection&#39;s chain and contract from its readable URL slug (e.g. eth / boredapeyachtclub).
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3SolanaCollectionTensorBid**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3solanacollectiontensorbid) | **Get** /v1/web3/solana/collections/{collection}/tensor-bids | A Solana collection’s Tensor bids (collection-wide and by trait), best first; price in lamports. Needs your Tensor key.
*Web3Api* | [**GetWeb3Token**](docs/Web3Api.md#getweb3token) | **Get** /v1/web3/tokens/{chain}/{contract}/{tokenId} | One NFT: metadata, traits, owner, open listings and offers, activity.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3Activity**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3activity) | **Get** /v1/web3/activity | Listings, offers, sales and cancels in the Crossly book, newest first.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3Alerts**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3alerts) | **Get** /v1/web3/alerts | Your NFT price alerts, with the cheapest open Crossly listing each one covers now.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3Approvals**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3approvals) | **Get** /v1/web3/approvals | Your collections the OpenSea conduit is approved to move, per linked wallet.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3Auctions**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3auctions) | **Get** /v1/web3/auctions | Live English auctions on the Crossly book, ending soonest first, each with its top bid and the least the next bid can be.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3BrowseCollections**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3browsecollections) | **Get** /v1/web3/browse/collections | Browse NFT collections by chain, category and name; sort hot (Crossly sales, listings, favourites), new, volume, floor or name.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3BrowseTokens**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3browsetokens) | **Get** /v1/web3/browse/tokens | Hot NFTs: what is for sale on Crossly first, then tokens from the hottest collections.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3Bundles**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3bundles) | **Get** /v1/web3/bundles | Open bundle listings (2–30 NFTs for one price), newest first, by chain or by a contract any item is from.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3CollectionHistory**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3collectionhistory) | **Get** /v1/web3/collections/{chain}/{contract}/history | Daily floor, sales, volume, holders and listed count for a collection, oldest first (up to 365 days).
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3Collections**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3collections) | **Get** /v1/web3/collections | Collections ranked by 30-day Crossly volume, with floor and listed count.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3CreatorCollections**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3creatorcollections) | **Get** /v1/web3/creator/collections | Collections you are the creator of: your launches, and contracts you claimed by owning them.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3Drops**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3drops) | **Get** /v1/web3/drops | Upcoming and live NFT drops (curated mints on the creator&#39;s own page), soonest first, with whether you asked to be reminded.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3HolderPerks**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3holderperks) | **Get** /v1/web3/holder-perks | Your holder perks: percent off your Crossly shop for holders of an NFT collection, best first.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3Launches**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3launches) | **Get** /v1/web3/launches | Your launchpad collections (drafts and live), newest first, with minted count and phase.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3Listings**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3listings) | **Get** /v1/web3/listings | Open NFT listings in the Crossly book — the cheapest per token.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3MarketplaceKeys**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3marketplacekeys) | **Get** /v1/web3/marketplace-keys | Your own marketplace API keys (last four characters only) and whether Crossly&#39;s key covers each marketplace.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3Nfts**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3nfts) | **Get** /v1/web3/nfts | List the NFTs in your inventory, imported from your linked wallets.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3Orders**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3orders) | **Get** /v1/web3/orders | Your orders in the Crossly book (listings and offers), any status.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3Purchases**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3purchases) | **Get** /v1/web3/purchases | NFTs your linked wallets bought — on Crossly or any marketplace the chain feed saw — newest first, with price and whether you still hold each.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3Rankings**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3rankings) | **Get** /v1/web3/rankings | Collections by sales volume over 24h, 7d or 30d, in one coin: the ETH chains together, or Polygon / Solana on their own.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3Reserved**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3reserved) | **Get** /v1/web3/reserved | Private listings reserved for one of your linked wallets — only you can see or buy them.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3Sales**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3sales) | **Get** /v1/web3/sales | The latest NFT sales across the directory, from the chain (Seaport and Blur) and the Crossly book, newest first; page with &#x60;before&#x60;.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3Search**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3search) | **Get** /v1/web3/search | Find collections by contract address (every chain) or by name.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3SearchTokens**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3searchtokens) | **Get** /v1/web3/search/tokens | Find tokens by collection name and number: &#39;pudgy 42&#39;, &#39;Bored Ape #1234&#39;.
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3Wallets**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3wallets) | **Get** /v1/web3/wallets | List your verified, linked wallets (EVM and Solana).
*Web3Api* | [**ListWeb3Watchlist**](docs/Web3Api.md#listweb3watchlist) | **Get** /v1/web3/watchlist | Your favourited NFTs, with the cheapest open Crossly listing and the last sale of each.
*Web3Api* | [**UpdateWeb3HolderPerk**](docs/Web3Api.md#updateweb3holderperk) | **Put** /v1/web3/holder-perks | Set the percent off (100–5000 bps) holders of a collection get across your Crossly shop; one perk per collection.
*Web3Api* | [**UpdateWeb3LaunchAllowlist**](docs/Web3Api.md#updateweb3launchallowlist) | **Put** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/allowlist | Replace the early-phase allowlist (up to 10,000 wallets). EVM live launches get back &#x60;rootTx&#x60;, the setAllowlistRoot transaction for the owner wallet.
*Web3Api* | [**UpdateWeb3MarketplaceKey**](docs/Web3Api.md#updateweb3marketplacekey) | **Put** /v1/web3/marketplace-keys/{marketplace} | Save your own API key for a marketplace (checked against the marketplace first where it can be); stored encrypted.
*WebhooksApi* | [**CreateWebhook**](docs/WebhooksApi.md#createwebhook) | **Post** /v1/webhooks | Register a webhook endpoint (returns the signing secret once).
*WebhooksApi* | [**CreateWebhookTest**](docs/WebhooksApi.md#createwebhooktest) | **Post** /v1/webhooks/{id}/test | Fire a synthetic test.ping delivery to one webhook.
*WebhooksApi* | [**DeleteWebhook**](docs/WebhooksApi.md#deletewebhook) | **Delete** /v1/webhooks/{id} | Delete a webhook endpoint.
*WebhooksApi* | [**GetWebhookStream**](docs/WebhooksApi.md#getwebhookstream) | **Get** /v1/webhooks/stream | Stream this account&#39;s webhook events as they happen (SSE).
*WebhooksApi* | [**ListWebhooks**](docs/WebhooksApi.md#listwebhooks) | **Get** /v1/webhooks | List your registered webhook endpoints.
*WorkflowsApi* | [**CreateWorkflowChain**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#createworkflowchain) | **Post** /v1/workflow-chains | Create a multi-step workflow chain.
*WorkflowsApi* | [**CreateWorkflowChainRunNow**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#createworkflowchainrunnow) | **Post** /v1/workflow-chains/{id}/run-now | Enqueue an ad-hoc run of a workflow chain.
*WorkflowsApi* | [**CreateWorkflowChainToggle**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#createworkflowchaintoggle) | **Post** /v1/workflow-chains/{id}/toggle | Flip a workflow chain between active and inactive.
*WorkflowsApi* | [**DeleteWorkflowChain**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#deleteworkflowchain) | **Delete** /v1/workflow-chains/{id} | Delete a workflow chain (cascades steps + runs).
*WorkflowsApi* | [**GetWorkflowChain**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#getworkflowchain) | **Get** /v1/workflow-chains/{id} | Get one workflow chain with its steps.
*WorkflowsApi* | [**ListWorkflowChains**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#listworkflowchains) | **Get** /v1/workflow-chains | List workflow chains with their step graph.
*WorkflowsApi* | [**UpdateWorkflowChain**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#updateworkflowchain) | **Put** /v1/workflow-chains/{id} | Replace a workflow chain wholesale.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateSpatialInviteAccept**](docs/WorldApi.md#createspatialinviteaccept) | **Post** /v1/spatial/invites/accept | Redeem an invite code (dashes optional); returns the room it opens.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateSpatialPropertyLeaseWithdraw**](docs/WorldApi.md#createspatialpropertyleasewithdraw) | **Post** /v1/spatial/property/leases/{id}/withdraw | Withdraw a lease offer nobody has accepted yet.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateSpatialScene**](docs/WorldApi.md#createspatialscene) | **Post** /v1/spatial/scenes | Start a 3D room: a collection (your items on shelves) or a warehouse (racks you arrange). Private until you make it public.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateSpatialSceneGrant**](docs/WorldApi.md#createspatialscenegrant) | **Post** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/grants | Let a specific user into one of your rooms (visitor or staff), no code needed.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateSpatialSceneInvite**](docs/WorldApi.md#createspatialsceneinvite) | **Post** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/invites | Mint an invite code to one of your rooms (visitor or staff, optional expiry in hours). The code is in this response only: just its hash is stored.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateSpatialSceneNode**](docs/WorldApi.md#createspatialscenenode) | **Post** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/nodes | Add a unit (rack, shelf, bin) to one of your warehouse rooms.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateSpatialScenePlace**](docs/WorldApi.md#createspatialsceneplace) | **Post** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/place | Pin an inventory item to a slot in one of your rooms: by warehouse nodeId, or by a collection container key and index.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateSpatialSceneRename**](docs/WorldApi.md#createspatialscenerename) | **Post** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/rename | Rename one of your rooms.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateSpatialSceneShopPlace**](docs/WorldApi.md#createspatialsceneshopplace) | **Post** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/shop/place | Place an inventory item in a section and display of your shop room.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateSpatialSceneUnpin**](docs/WorldApi.md#createspatialsceneunpin) | **Post** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/unpin | Clear every pin in one of your rooms, so it arranges itself again.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateSpatialSceneVisibility**](docs/WorldApi.md#createspatialscenevisibility) | **Post** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/visibility | Make one of your rooms private, unlisted (anyone with the link) or public (in the lobby and searchable).
*WorldApi* | [**CreateSpatialSceneWarehouseStock**](docs/WorldApi.md#createspatialscenewarehousestock) | **Post** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/warehouse/stock | Put an inventory item (or unit) on a rack in one of your warehouse rooms.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateSpatialShopNeighbourhood**](docs/WorldApi.md#createspatialshopneighbourhood) | **Post** /v1/spatial/shop/neighbourhood | Move your shop to another neighbourhood (by slug).
*WorldApi* | [**CreateSpatialStreetApply**](docs/WorldApi.md#createspatialstreetapply) | **Post** /v1/spatial/streets/{slug}/apply | Ask to open your shop on a street whose owner reviews applications.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateSpatialStreetInvite**](docs/WorldApi.md#createspatialstreetinvite) | **Post** /v1/spatial/streets/{slug}/invite | Invite a shop (by its slug) to open on a street you own.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldAvatarEquip**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldavatarequip) | **Post** /v1/world/avatar/equip | Wear something you already own -- exactly ONE of { cosmeticId } (a cosmetic you hold), { receiptId } (a Crossly purchase from your closet) or { inventoryItemId } (your own stock). The slot comes from the thing itself; slot is only a fallback for an item with no catalog shape. Replaces whatever was in that slot. Moves no money. Answers the slot and your loadout after the change.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldAvatarUnequip**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldavatarunequip) | **Post** /v1/world/avatar/unequip | Take off whatever is in one slot. Idempotent: an empty slot answers removed:false. Answers your loadout after the change.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldAvatarWearable**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldavatarwearable) | **Post** /v1/world/avatar/wearables | Author a wearable for a body slot (a mesh profile and params, an asking price in COSM base units as a string). A submission: inactive until an operator approves it.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldContest**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldcontest) | **Post** /v1/world/contests | Set up a skill contest on a game placed in your place: title, game (its instance id), entry { mode, feeCents? }, prizes by place, funder, window, attempts. Paid entry needs the operator to allow it where it runs.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldContestEntrySettlePayment**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldcontestentrysettlepayment) | **Post** /v1/world/contests/entries/{id}/settle-payment | Read the fee of a paid entry back from Stripe and settle it (safe to repeat).
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldContestJoin**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldcontestjoin) | **Post** /v1/world/contests/{id}/join | Enter a contest. Free and sponsored contests take you in at once; a paid one answers a client secret to pay the entry fee.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldConvention**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldconvention) | **Post** /v1/world/conventions | Host a convention in one of your rooms: title, dates, tables and how sellers get one (invite, free, a fee in cents, or a revenue share in basis points).
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldConventionAccept**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldconventionaccept) | **Post** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/accept | Accept: your own invitation (no body), or, as host, an application (username).
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldConventionApply**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldconventionapply) | **Post** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/apply | Ask for a table at a convention.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldConventionInvite**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldconventioninvite) | **Post** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/invite | Invite a seller (by shop handle) to a convention you host.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldConventionPaid**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldconventionpaid) | **Post** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/paid | As host, record that a seller (username) paid their table fee off-platform. Moves no money.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldConventionWithdraw**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldconventionwithdraw) | **Post** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/withdraw | Withdraw your table or decline your invitation; as host, decline someone (username).
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldCrew**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldcrew) | **Post** /v1/world/crews | Start a crew you own: { name (2-32 chars), tag (2-5 letters or digits, shown on members&#39; nameplates) }. Answers its id and join code. 409 when you are already in a crew.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldCrewChat**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldcrewchat) | **Post** /v1/world/crew/chat | Say a line to your crew (body: text, up to 400 characters). Returns data.messages, the crew chat ring (the last 50 lines, kept in memory only), and data.said when your line went out; a blocked or too-quick line is dropped silently. 404 when you are in no crew.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldCrewJoin**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldcrewjoin) | **Post** /v1/world/crews/join | Join a crew by the code a member gave you. 404 for an unknown code, 409 when you are already in a crew or it is full (30).
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldCrewLeave**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldcrewleave) | **Post** /v1/world/crews/leave | Leave your crew. If you own it, the crew is disbanded. Idempotent: leaving when in none succeeds.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldDecorDesign**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworlddecordesign) | **Post** /v1/world/decor/designs | Author a decor design (what it unlocks, a mesh profile and params, an asking price in COSM base units as a string). Inactive until an operator approves it.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldDecorDesignSubmit**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworlddecordesignsubmit) | **Post** /v1/world/decor/designs/{id}/submit | Send one of your decor designs to the review queue.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldDecorRetract**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworlddecorretract) | **Post** /v1/world/decor/retract | Take a placed decor piece back into your inventory; answers the inventory after.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldDm**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworlddm) | **Post** /v1/world/dms/{userId} | Send a direct message to a friend (a mutual follow; never across a block), up to 400 characters, through the world chat guard. 403 when they are not a friend, 429 when sent too quickly.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldFollow**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldfollow) | **Post** /v1/world/follows/{userId} | Follow somebody. When they follow you back you are friends (friends-here, invites, messages). Idempotent.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldFriendInvite**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldfriendinvite) | **Post** /v1/world/friends/{userId}/invite | \&quot;Join me\&quot;: send one friend (a mutual follow) a notification with a link to the PUBLIC room you name by slug. 404 when they are not a friend or the room is not public; 429 when you invited them in the last minute.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldGamePackage**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldgamepackage) | **Post** /v1/world/games/packages | Submit a game package version for review: files as { path, base64 } (game.json, thumb.png, logic/, scene/, audio/, images/). Checked like the CLI, assets quarantined, the entry document dry-run, then reviewed by a person.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldGamePackageCheck**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldgamepackagecheck) | **Post** /v1/world/games/packages/check | Validate a game package exactly as a submission is checked (the shared intake report, your version still free) without storing, uploading or queuing anything: world-kit validate for an agent. Same files body as submit.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldGameRating**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldgamerating) | **Post** /v1/world/games/{id}/rating | Rate a game you played, 1 to 5 stars (rating again replaces it). Only players the runner recorded in that game can rate it.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldGameReport**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldgamereport) | **Post** /v1/world/games/{id}/report | Report a game: reason broken | unfair | offensive | scam | other, optional detail. Staff review it; the creator is told the reason, never who.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldGameStoreCheckout**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldgamestorecheckout) | **Post** /v1/world/games/{id}/store/{item}/checkout | Buy an item or pass from a game store: answers a client secret for the payment; the item is yours once it succeeds.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldGrailMatch**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldgrailmatch) | **Post** /v1/world/grails/match | Grail glints: send the ids on show somewhere (listingIds -- listings.id, inventoryIds, skuIds -- catalogue market SKUs; up to 400 each) and get back data.listingIds / data.inventoryIds / data.skuIds: the ones that are things you already want -- an open bid, a watched or wishlisted listing (or one selling the same SKU), a market watch, or a card your deck is missing. Read-only.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldHallLoan**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldhallloan) | **Post** /v1/world/hall/loans | Lend a piece from your vault to this week&#39;s Hall of Grails exhibition: { vaultItemId, credit: &#39;name&#39; | &#39;anonymous&#39; }. It shows under your plaque until the exhibition ends; it stays in your vault (still listable and withdrawable), and stops showing if it leaves.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldHomeAsk**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldhomeask) | **Post** /v1/world/homes/{slug}/ask | Ask a home owner whether they would sell a piece you saw there (an item or a decor piece).
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldHomeGuestbook**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldhomeguestbook) | **Post** /v1/world/homes/{slug}/guestbook | Sign a home guestbook (one short line).
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldHomeGuestbookReport**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldhomeguestbookreport) | **Post** /v1/world/homes/{slug}/guestbook/{id}/report | Report a guestbook entry to moderation.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldHuntStart**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldhuntstart) | **Post** /v1/world/hunt/start | Start the Great Hunt this season by reading its first riddle (the Copper key&#39;s). Idempotent: starting again answers your progress unchanged. Answers the same shape as GET /v1/world/hunt. 409 when no season is running.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldInteractiveAsset**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldinteractiveasset) | **Post** /v1/world/interactive/assets | Upload one asset for your designs: kind glb (4 MB), wav (4 MB) or png (1 MB), the file as base64. Answers its sha256 and the path to reference from MML.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldInteractiveDesign**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldinteractivedesign) | **Post** /v1/world/interactive/designs | Create an interactive design: name, footprint (s, m, l or f), optional tint and description, an asking price in COSM base units (a string, six decimals) and optional max supply. Inactive until ops approve a submitted version.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldInteractiveDesignCheck**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldinteractivedesigncheck) | **Post** /v1/world/interactive/designs/{id}/check | Check the draft: static rules, that every referenced upload exists, and a sandboxed dry run. Budgeted per person.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldInteractiveDesignPreview**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldinteractivedesignpreview) | **Post** /v1/world/interactive/designs/{id}/preview | A private preview token for the draft (author only) and the socket path that runs it. Budgeted per person.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldInteractiveDesignSubmit**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldinteractivedesignsubmit) | **Post** /v1/world/interactive/designs/{id}/submit | Freeze the draft as a new version and send it to ops review. Up to 10 a day.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldInteractiveReset**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldinteractivereset) | **Post** /v1/world/interactive/reset | Wipe the stored state of an interactive piece standing in a place you own (a home or your boutique).
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldJobAppraisalAnswer**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldjobappraisalanswer) | **Post** /v1/world/jobs/appraisals/{id}/answers | Answer a price check: your estimate of the item value in CENTS and an optional note. Moves no money; the asker may accept it.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldJobShiftClaim**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldjobshiftclaim) | **Post** /v1/world/jobs/shifts/{id}/claim | Claim an open shift. You work it by being in the place while it runs.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldJobShiftRelease**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldjobshiftrelease) | **Post** /v1/world/jobs/shifts/{id}/release | Give a shift you claimed back to the board.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldOutfit**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldoutfit) | **Post** /v1/world/outfits | Save what you are wearing now as a named look: { name (1-32 chars), appearance? (skin, hair, hairColour, eyes, build -- unknown keys fall back to the default) }. Each worn thing is recorded as the door it goes back on by; one that no longer traces to something you own is listed in skipped and not saved. 409 when you already keep 6.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldOutfitApply**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldoutfitapply) | **Post** /v1/world/outfits/{id}/apply | Put a saved look on. Every item goes through the same equip as POST /v1/world/avatar/equip, so ownership still holds: something you no longer own is listed in skipped with the reason and not worn. Slots the look left empty are emptied (cleared). Answers the appearance of the look (for your character -- appearance lives on your device), applied, skipped and cleared. Moves no money.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldParty**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldparty) | **Post** /v1/world/parties | Start a party with you as leader (up to 8 members).
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldPartyChat**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldpartychat) | **Post** /v1/world/parties/{id}/chat | Say something in your party chat (up to 2000 characters).
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldPartyInvite**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldpartyinvite) | **Post** /v1/world/parties/{id}/invite | Invite a friend (userId) to your party.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldPartyInviteAccept**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldpartyinviteaccept) | **Post** /v1/world/parties/invites/{inviteId}/accept | Accept a party invite.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldPartyInviteDecline**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldpartyinvitedecline) | **Post** /v1/world/parties/invites/{inviteId}/decline | Decline a party invite.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldPartyKick**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldpartykick) | **Post** /v1/world/parties/{id}/kick | As leader, remove a member (userId).
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldPartyLeader**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldpartyleader) | **Post** /v1/world/parties/{id}/leader | As leader, pass the lead to a member (userId).
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldPartyLeave**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldpartyleave) | **Post** /v1/world/parties/{id}/leave | Leave your party (the lead passes on if you led it).
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldPartyWishlist**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldpartywishlist) | **Post** /v1/world/parties/{id}/wishlist | Add a listing (listingId, or its shop slug) to the party wishlist; answers the wishlist.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldPartyWishlistVote**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldpartywishlistvote) | **Post** /v1/world/parties/{id}/wishlist/{listingId}/vote | Thumb a wishlist listing up or down (null clears your vote); answers the wishlist.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldPlaceItemWant**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldplaceitemwant) | **Post** /v1/world/places/{slug}/items/{itemId}/want | Tell a room owner you would buy one of their items (once per person per item; they see the count). Moves no money.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldSpace**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldspace) | **Post** /v1/world/spaces | Open a Space (sellers only, at most two): slug, name, venue, optional membership and about.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldSpaceEvent**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldspaceevent) | **Post** /v1/world/spaces/{slug}/events | As host or co-host, put an event on: title, kind, optional about, ISO start and end with offset.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldSpaceEventRsvp**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldspaceeventrsvp) | **Post** /v1/world/spaces/{slug}/events/{id}/rsvp | Say whether you are going to an event.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldSpaceInvite**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldspaceinvite) | **Post** /v1/world/spaces/{slug}/invite | Invite somebody (userId) to a Space.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldSpaceJoin**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldspacejoin) | **Post** /v1/world/spaces/{slug}/join | Join a Space, or ask to when it is request-only.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldSpaceLeave**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldspaceleave) | **Post** /v1/world/spaces/{slug}/leave | Leave a Space.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldSpaceRequestByUserId**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldspacerequestbyuserid) | **Post** /v1/world/spaces/{slug}/requests/{userId} | As host or co-host, accept or turn down a request to join.
*WorldApi* | [**CreateWorldSpaceRole**](docs/WorldApi.md#createworldspacerole) | **Post** /v1/world/spaces/{slug}/role | As host, make a member a co-host or back to a member.
*WorldApi* | [**DeleteSpatialSceneGrant**](docs/WorldApi.md#deletespatialscenegrant) | **Delete** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/grants/{grantId} | Take somebody&#39;s access to your room away, or kill an invite nobody has used yet.
*WorldApi* | [**DeleteSpatialSceneNode**](docs/WorldApi.md#deletespatialscenenode) | **Delete** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/nodes | Remove units (by id, up to 500) from one of your warehouse rooms.
*WorldApi* | [**DeleteSpatialSceneWarehouseStock**](docs/WorldApi.md#deletespatialscenewarehousestock) | **Delete** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/warehouse/stock | Take an inventory item (or unit) off its rack.
*WorldApi* | [**DeleteSpatialStreetRequest**](docs/WorldApi.md#deletespatialstreetrequest) | **Delete** /v1/spatial/streets/requests/{id} | Withdraw a street request you made that has not been founded yet.
*WorldApi* | [**DeleteWorldBlock**](docs/WorldApi.md#deleteworldblock) | **Delete** /v1/world/blocks/{id} | Unblock somebody (by the block id from GET /v1/world/blocks).
*WorldApi* | [**DeleteWorldCollectionCapability**](docs/WorldApi.md#deleteworldcollectioncapability) | **Delete** /v1/world/collections/{chain}/{contract}/capabilities | Withdraw your declaration for a collection.
*WorldApi* | [**DeleteWorldFollow**](docs/WorldApi.md#deleteworldfollow) | **Delete** /v1/world/follows/{userId} | Unfollow somebody. Idempotent.
*WorldApi* | [**DeleteWorldHallLoan**](docs/WorldApi.md#deleteworldhallloan) | **Delete** /v1/world/hall/loans/{id} | Take one of your loans off show in the Hall of Grails.
*WorldApi* | [**DeleteWorldHomeGuestbook**](docs/WorldApi.md#deleteworldhomeguestbook) | **Delete** /v1/world/homes/{slug}/guestbook/{id} | As the home owner, hide a guestbook entry.
*WorldApi* | [**DeleteWorldInteractiveDesign**](docs/WorldApi.md#deleteworldinteractivedesign) | **Delete** /v1/world/interactive/designs/{id} | Delete a draft design, or archive a submitted one (off sale; owned copies keep working).
*WorldApi* | [**DeleteWorldJobShift**](docs/WorldApi.md#deleteworldjobshift) | **Delete** /v1/world/jobs/shifts/{id} | Cancel a shift you posted that nobody has worked yet.
*WorldApi* | [**DeleteWorldOutfit**](docs/WorldApi.md#deleteworldoutfit) | **Delete** /v1/world/outfits/{id} | Delete a saved look. 404 when it is not yours.
*WorldApi* | [**DeleteWorldPartyWishlist**](docs/WorldApi.md#deleteworldpartywishlist) | **Delete** /v1/world/parties/{id}/wishlist/{listingId} | Take a listing you added off the party wishlist; answers the wishlist.
*WorldApi* | [**DeleteWorldPlaceBan**](docs/WorldApi.md#deleteworldplaceban) | **Delete** /v1/world/places/{slug}/bans/{userId} | Lift a ban from a place you own; they can walk in again right away.
*WorldApi* | [**DeleteWorldSpace**](docs/WorldApi.md#deleteworldspace) | **Delete** /v1/world/spaces/{slug} | As host, close a Space.
*WorldApi* | [**DeleteWorldSpaceEvent**](docs/WorldApi.md#deleteworldspaceevent) | **Delete** /v1/world/spaces/{slug}/events/{id} | As host or co-host, cancel an event.
*WorldApi* | [**GetSpatialSceneAttention**](docs/WorldApi.md#getspatialsceneattention) | **Get** /v1/spatial/scenes/{sceneId}/attention | What visitors looked at in one of your rooms: viewers, looks, inspects and dwell per item over the last days (1-35, default 30).
*WorldApi* | [**GetSpatialSceneWindow**](docs/WorldApi.md#getspatialscenewindow) | **Get** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/window | The listings you chose for the street window of your shop, in slot order (left, middle, right; empty &#x3D; newest first), and &#x60;choices&#x60;: your listings that can go in it, newest first.
*WorldApi* | [**GetSpatialShopNeighbourhood**](docs/WorldApi.md#getspatialshopneighbourhood) | **Get** /v1/spatial/shop/neighbourhood | Your shop&#39;s neighbourhood, and whether and where it may move.
*WorldApi* | [**GetSpatialSpace**](docs/WorldApi.md#getspatialspace) | **Get** /v1/spatial/spaces | The room catalogue: every space profile (what it holds, how it is sorted, its containers) and the warehouse units.
*WorldApi* | [**GetSpatialStreetAdmission**](docs/WorldApi.md#getspatialstreetadmission) | **Get** /v1/spatial/streets/admissions | Street applications and invitations: yours, and those waiting on streets you own.
*WorldApi* | [**GetSpatialStreetDecor**](docs/WorldApi.md#getspatialstreetdecor) | **Get** /v1/spatial/streets/{slug}/decor | The decor on a street you may dress, and what you can place.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldArcade**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldarcade) | **Get** /v1/world/arcade | The Arcade in Crossly World: data.cabinets (slot, where it stands, host -- piece, price_guess or empty --, game, title, blurb, the piece it hosts and where, record, packageId and thumbnail for a creator game from the Game Directory, players) and data.scores (the High Score wall: per game a title, kind -- leaderboard, record or top -- and up to five ASCII lines; a name shows only for somebody who chose to be seen by name, everyone else is \&quot;A player\&quot;), plus creatorGames (creator games with a cabinet now). Games are played in the world.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldAurora**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldaurora) | **Get** /v1/world/aurora | The Sales Aurora over the Commons in Crossly World: every real sale on Crossly in the last data.windowDays (30) days. data.total (sales in the window), data.families (key, label, colour, count per colour family: cards, coins, jewellery, electronics, media, fashion, toys, other) and data.sales (newest first, at most 48: family, ageHours, fresh -- sold in the last ten minutes, which shimmers). Aggregate only: no item, price, seller or platform. data.updatedAt is when it was read.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldAutoShop**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldautoshop) | **Get** /v1/world/auto-shop | Whether Crossly keeps a world shop for you automatically: enabled, and the shop (id, name, visibility, autoCreated, address, label) or null. Change it with PATCH /v1/world/auto-shop.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldAvatarIdentity**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldavataridentity) | **Get** /v1/world/avatar/identity | Whether others see your name in the world (named), or not (anonymous); null if you have never chosen.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldAvatarLoadout**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldavatarloadout) | **Get** /v1/world/avatar/loadout | What you are wearing: the rig, its slots (avatar_head, avatar_body, avatar_outer -- a jacket over the top, avatar_legs, avatar_feet, avatar_back) and each worn entry -- a cosmetic or a real item -- with its verified-purchase mark. Carries no price, receipt id, seller or order.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldAvatarLoadoutByUserId**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldavatarloadoutbyuserid) | **Get** /v1/world/avatar/loadout/{userId} | What somebody else is wearing, with the verified-purchase mark on each real item -- public on purpose, since a check only its owner can see is not a check. No price, receipt id, seller or order.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldAvatarLook**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldavatarlook) | **Get** /v1/world/avatar/look | Your world look on the account: the handle you chose (null if anonymous) and your appearance; null if you never made one.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldAvatarNftBadge**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldavatarnftbadge) | **Get** /v1/world/avatar/nft-badge | The NFT you wear on your nameplate in the world, or null.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldCapability**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldcapability) | **Get** /v1/world/capabilities | What your held NFTs unlock in Crossly World: each token with the capabilities its collection declared (wear, display, emote, gate, ...). Holding is your verified wallets (their synced tokens); the capabilities of a transferred token go within five minutes.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldCapabilityCatalogue**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldcapabilitycatalogue) | **Get** /v1/world/capabilities/catalogue | The capability kinds of spec crossly.nft-capabilities/1: what each does, whether only the creator of the collection may declare it, whether it needs review, and which clients honour it.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldCapabilityWear**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldcapabilitywear) | **Get** /v1/world/capabilities/wear | What you wear from your NFTs and COSM copies: your pick per avatar slot (head, body, legs, feet, back) and what the room sees.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldChest**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldchest) | **Get** /v1/world/chests | The Commons chest: data.window (this 20-minute window, UTC), spot ([x, z] on the plaza), endsAt (when the next chest comes) and mine (openedThis, and opened -- how many windows you have opened one in; the Chest opener pin comes at 10). A chest is opened by walking up to it in Crossly World, never over the API.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldCollectionCapability**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldcollectioncapability) | **Get** /v1/world/collections/{chain}/{contract}/capabilities | The live capability declaration of a collection (404 when it has none).
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldContestMine**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldcontestmine) | **Get** /v1/world/contests/mine | Your contest entries (best score, attempts used, payment state) and prizes (granted, or owed until their gate opens).
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldConvention**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldconvention) | **Get** /v1/world/conventions/{id} | One convention. 404 for an unknown or malformed id alike.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldCosmBalance**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldcosmbalance) | **Get** /v1/world/cosm/balance | Your COSM balance: baseUnits (a STRING -- COSM has 6 decimals and overflows a JSON number), decimals, available (false &#x3D; COSM is not provisioned on this deployment, not a zero balance).
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldCrew**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldcrew) | **Get** /v1/world/crew | Your crew: data.crew is null when you are in none, else its name, tag, join code and whether you own it; data.members lists up to 30 members, each with the public room they are in right now when they chose to be seen by name (else null).
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldCrewChat**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldcrewchat) | **Get** /v1/world/crew/chat | Your crew chat: data.messages is the last 50 lines (from, name, text, at), kept in memory only, so it starts empty after a restart. 404 when you are in no crew.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldCrewLeaderboard**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldcrewleaderboard) | **Get** /v1/world/crew/leaderboards | The game leaderboards (Price Guess, hunts, speed trades; this week and all time) ranked among your crew only, each member by the name the crew sees. 404 when you are in no crew.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldDm**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworlddm) | **Get** /v1/world/dms/{userId} | Your direct-message thread with one friend, oldest first (the last 100 lines: id, from, text, at, mine). Reading it marks their lines read.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldEmoteShowable**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldemoteshowable) | **Get** /v1/world/emotes/showable | The things you own that a show-off emote can hold up.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldFirefly**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldfirefly) | **Get** /v1/world/fireflies | Fireflies on the Commons lawn: data.day (today, UTC), today (fireflies everyone has caught today) and mine (today -- your catches today, up to perDay -- total, all your catches; the Firefly Catcher pin comes at 30 -- and perDay). Fireflies are caught by walking up to a lit one in Crossly World after dark, never over the API, and nothing is spent.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldFitStat**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldfitstat) | **Get** /v1/world/fits/stats | Your walking storefront, last 7 days: distinct people who tapped \&quot;Shop this\&quot; or \&quot;Offer\&quot; on what you wore, and how many of each. Counts only; nobody is identified.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldFitValue**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldfitvalue) | **Get** /v1/world/fit-value | What your outfit is worth on the market: cents (sum of each catalogued piece&#39;s order-book price -- last sale, else lowest ask), pieces worn, how many were priced, and share (whether you show it on your card).
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldGame**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldgame) | **Get** /v1/world/games/{id} | One live game: its manifest, its store items and which you own.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldGameLeaderboard**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldgameleaderboard) | **Get** /v1/world/games/{id}/leaderboard | A live game leaderboard, this week and all time, by its manifest scoring (wins, best points, or best time). Only people who chose to be named show by name. scoring is null for a game that keeps no board.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldGamePackage**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldgamepackage) | **Get** /v1/world/games/packages/{id} | One of your game packages with every version, its review state and decision note.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldGameTemplate**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldgametemplate) | **Get** /v1/world/games/templates/{kind} | A fresh game package from a template (table, arena, object; collection is an NFT capability declaration), as { path, base64 } files: the world-kit init, for an agent with no local kit. ?id&#x3D;my-game&amp;name&#x3D;My Game.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldHall**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldhall) | **Get** /v1/world/hall | The Hall of Grails in Crossly World: data.exhibition (key, title, blurb, endsAt -- a new one every Monday UTC -- and next, the coming one&#39;s title) and data.exhibits, up to twelve real catalogue items in slot order (skuId, name, brand, imageUrl, thumbUrl, retailCents, lowestAskCents, lastSaleCents: the live order book, null when the market has none). The first stands on the centre plinth. Buy, bid or watch an exhibit through the market endpoints with its skuId.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldHallLoan**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldhallloan) | **Get** /v1/world/hall/loans | Lend to the Hall: this week&#39;s exhibition (key, title, categorySlug, endsAt), your loans on show (id, vaultItemId, skuId, name, credit, since) and the vault pieces you can lend (eligible: vaultItemId, skuId, name, imageUrl, grade) -- held in your Crossly vault, not pledged, in the exhibition&#39;s category. perMember is how many you may lend each week.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldHomePlaceable**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldhomeplaceable) | **Get** /v1/world/home/placeable | What you can place in your home: your items and your furniture.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldHunt**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldhunt) | **Get** /v1/world/hunt | Your Great Hunt: data.season (id, name, startsAt, endsAt, the title and how many earn it, live), started (you read the first clue), held (keys held, 0-3, always in the order copper, jade, crystal), keys (each with its name, colour, its riddle once it is yours to read, and when you picked it up), and cleared (your rank through the Gate this season, when, and whether you earned the title) or null. Keys are picked up by walking to them in Crossly World; nothing here takes one.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldHuntScoreboard**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldhuntscoreboard) | **Get** /v1/world/hunt/scoreboard | The Great Hunt Scoreboard, as the monolith in the Commons shows it: data.season (the running season, or the last one between seasons), finishers (the first ten through the Gate in order: rank, name, short, named, at, titled -- a name shows only for somebody who chose to be seen by name in the world, everyone else is \&quot;A player\&quot;) and total (everybody through the Gate this season).
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldInteractiveDesign**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldinteractivedesign) | **Get** /v1/world/interactive/designs/{id} | One of your interactive designs: its draft source, the last static check, and every submitted version with its review state.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldJob**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldjob) | **Get** /v1/world/jobs | The jobs board: open staff shifts you could claim, your claimed shifts and open price checks (appraisals), as the world shows them.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldKite**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldkite) | **Get** /v1/world/kites | Kites flown on the Commons lawn: data.day (today, UTC), today (kites everyone has flown today) and mine (today -- yours today, up to perDay -- perDay, and days -- the days you have flown one on; the Kite flyer pin comes at 5). Kites are flown by standing out on the lawn in Crossly World by day, never over the API, and nothing is spent.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldLantern**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldlantern) | **Get** /v1/world/lanterns | Sky lanterns in the Commons: data.day (today, UTC), today (lanterns everyone has released today) and mine (today -- yours today, up to perDay -- perDay, and days -- the days you have released one on; the Lantern lighter pin comes at 5). Lanterns are released at the lantern stand in Crossly World, never over the API, and nothing is spent.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldPartyChat**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldpartychat) | **Get** /v1/world/parties/{id}/chat | Your party chat: the recent lines. 404 when this is not your party.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldPlaceBoard**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldplaceboard) | **Get** /v1/world/places/{slug}/boards | The Trade Floor&#39;s wall of wants, its authentication desk and its Vault Markets ticker (vaultMovers: bookKey, name, label, units, markCents, change24hBps; only the trade-floor place has them).
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldPlaceCollection**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldplacecollection) | **Get** /v1/world/places/{slug}/collections/{category}/{brand}/{model} | One collection room: every piece of the set, owned or missing. 404 for an unknown room or set.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldPlaceDrop**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldplacedrop) | **Get** /v1/world/places/{slug}/drop | The owner&#39;s next drop (name, cover, time, live) and how many people are waiting in the room.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldPlaceItemInterest**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldplaceiteminterest) | **Get** /v1/world/places/{slug}/items/{itemId}/interest | How many people looked at one item in this room today.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldPlaceLive**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldplacelive) | **Get** /v1/world/places/{slug}/live | The owner&#39;s live sale: their stream and the lot on the block, or null.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldPlaceSeller**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldplaceseller) | **Get** /v1/world/places/{slug}/seller | The shop whose public room this is (username and name), or null.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldPlaceShopPet**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldplaceshoppet) | **Get** /v1/world/places/{slug}/shop-pet | The shop&#39;s pet kind, or null.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldSpace**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldspace) | **Get** /v1/world/spaces/{slug} | One Space: its layout, host, events and your place in it.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldSpaceCanHost**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldspacecanhost) | **Get** /v1/world/spaces/can-host | Whether you may open a Space: sellers only, at most two active.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldTrialLeaderboard**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldtrialleaderboard) | **Get** /v1/world/trials/{course}/leaderboard | A Time Trials course&#39;s boards, as the board beside it in the Commons shows them: data.course, week, weekly (this week&#39;s ten fastest: rank, name, short, named, ms, at) and allTime (the ten fastest ever, each person once). A name shows only for somebody who chose to be seen by name in the world; everyone else is \&quot;A player\&quot;. 404 for an unknown course; course 1 is commons-loop.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldTrialMine**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldtrialmine) | **Get** /v1/world/trials/mine | Your Hoverboard Time Trials bests: data.week (its Monday, YYYY-MM-DD, UTC) and courses, each with course (id, name, parMs, rings, lengthM), week (your best lap this week in ms, your place on the weekly board, laps finished, and the split at each ring) or null, and allTime (your best ever in ms, your place on the all-time board, when) or null. Laps are timed by Crossly from where it saw you ride in the world; nothing here writes one.
*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldWallet**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldwallet) | **Get** /v1/world/wallet | Every balance you hold in Crossly World, read-only: bucksCents (Crossly Bucks, cents); cbx (available, decimals, and the three CBX balances kept apart as base-unit STRINGS -- granted (spend here only), earned (withdrawable once matured), connected (a delegation ceiling on your own wallet, not cash on hand) -- plus pendingCents of cashback not yet matured); cosm (available, decimals, baseUnits string). available:false means that currency is not provisioned on this deployment, not that you hold none.
