*WorldApi* | [**GetWorldWish**](docs/WorldApi.md#getworldwish) | **Get** /v1/world/wishes | The Wishing Fountain in the Commons: data.day (today, UTC), today (wishes everyone has made today) and mine (madeToday, days -- how many days you have wished on, the Wishmaker pin comes at 7 -- and nextAt, when tomorrow&#39;s wish opens). One wish a day; a wish is made by tossing a coin at the fountain in Crossly World, never over the API, and nothing is spent.
*WorldApi* | [**ListSpatialSceneGrants**](docs/WorldApi.md#listspatialscenegrants) | **Get** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/grants | Everyone who can currently walk into one of your rooms, and unused invites. Never returns codes.
*WorldApi* | [**ListSpatialSceneWarehouseUnplaced**](docs/WorldApi.md#listspatialscenewarehouseunplaced) | **Get** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/warehouse/unplaced | Stock not yet on a shelf in a warehouse room you can arrange (q filters by title).
*WorldApi* | [**ListSpatialSharedWithMe**](docs/WorldApi.md#listspatialsharedwithme) | **Get** /v1/spatial/shared-with-me | Rooms other people let you into.
*WorldApi* | [**ListSpatialStreetLeases**](docs/WorldApi.md#listspatialstreetleases) | **Get** /v1/spatial/streets/{slug}/leases | Plots on a street offered for lease: weekly rent in CENTS and terms.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldAvatarCloset**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldavatarcloset) | **Get** /v1/world/avatar/closet | Everything you can verifiably wear (your Crossly purchase receipts), newest first: receiptId, item title, brand, size, image, catalog sku/variant, quantity, purchasedAt. Pass a receiptId to POST /v1/world/avatar/equip to put one on. limit 1-200, offset for paging.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldAvatarStock**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldavatarstock) | **Get** /v1/world/avatar/stock | Items of your OWN inventory you could wear into a room as a walking storefront: inventoryItemId, title, image and the slot it would go on. Pass an inventoryItemId to POST /v1/world/avatar/equip.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldAvatarWearables**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldavatarwearables) | **Get** /v1/world/avatar/wearables | Wearables you have authored as a designer: id, sku, name, slot, priceBaseUnits (COSM base units, a string), maxSupply, mintedCount, active (false until reviewed). meta.available is false when COSM is not provisioned here.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldBestDressed**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldbestdressed) | **Get** /v1/world/best-dressed | This week&#39;s best-dressed board (Monday-based), top ten: name, profile username and loves. Only people still choosing to be seen by name.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldCapabilityDeclarations**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldcapabilitydeclarations) | **Get** /v1/world/capabilities/declarations | Your capability declarations, in every review state (pending, live, rejected with the note from the reviewer).
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldContestStandings**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldconteststandings) | **Get** /v1/world/contests/{id}/standings | The standings of a contest now: best score first (or lowest, for a time game), ties to whoever got there first.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldContests**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldcontests) | **Get** /v1/world/contests | Open skill contests on Crossly World games, soonest ending first: entry mode (free, sponsored, paid) and prizes.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldConventionExhibitors**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldconventionexhibitors) | **Get** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/exhibitors | The host roster: who applied, who was invited, who is coming (by shop handle). Host only; 404 otherwise.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldConventionMine**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldconventionmine) | **Get** /v1/world/conventions/mine | Conventions you host and conventions you are exhibiting at.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldConventions**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldconventions) | **Get** /v1/world/conventions | Conventions open to sellers now or soon: title, room, dates, and how to get a table (invite, free, fee or revenue share).
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldCosmCosmetics**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldcosmcosmetics) | **Get** /v1/world/cosm/cosmetics | The active cosmetics catalogue, optionally by tier (premium | earned | stake_exclusive) or slot: sku, name, slot, tier, priceCents (premium only), priceBaseUnits (earned only, COSM base units string), supply, fusable, preview and what it unlocks. Read-only: cosmetics are bought on crossly.net. meta.available / meta.decimals.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldCosmDesigns**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldcosmdesigns) | **Get** /v1/world/cosm/designs | Your COSM designs in the world: popularity toward the free chain unlock a popular limited design earns, whether it is on chain, and your opt-out. Declare what a design does with chain cosm and the design id as the contract.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldCosmInventory**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldcosminventory) | **Get** /v1/world/cosm/inventory | Cosmetics you hold, newest first: ownershipId, cosmeticId, sku, name, slot, tier, how it was acquired, bound, edition number, acquiredAt and what it unlocks. Pass a cosmeticId to POST /v1/world/avatar/equip to wear one. meta.available.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldDecorCatalogue**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworlddecorcatalogue) | **Get** /v1/world/decor/catalogue | The Decor Shop: every active cosmetic grouped by surface (avatar | home | shop | stall | street) and slot -- name, tier, priceCents (premium: the dollar price, paid in CBX at spot) or priceBaseUnits (earned: COSM, base-unit string), edition supply and remaining, tint swatch, preview, what it unlocks, owned + your edition number, and buyWith. Read-only: buying and minting happen on crossly.net/shop/decor. meta.available, meta.mint {open, reason}, meta.cbx {open, reason} (CBX opens at mint), meta.balances (your COSM and CBX, base-unit strings), meta.surfaces. Never sold by card.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldDecorDesigns**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworlddecordesigns) | **Get** /v1/world/decor/designs | Your decor designs and their review state. available is false until COSM is provisioned.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldDecorInventory**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworlddecorinventory) | **Get** /v1/world/decor/inventory | Your decor as an INVENTORY. Decor is a quantity: every copy is one unit -- a placed prop (home_prop, prop) uses one per placement, a finish (wallpaper, floor, theme, shopfront, facade, sign style, street lamp/banner/paving/arch, booth canopy/pattern/lights/counter) one per space it dresses; free values are unlimited. Per item: key, value, pair (key:value), name, sku, surface, placeable, tint, owned, placed, available, placements [{ space { kind: home | shop | stall | street, id, name }, count }], copies [{ ownershipId, editionNumber }]. meta.available, meta.spaces (every space you dress). Read-only: placing and retracting happen in the app or on crossly.net.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldDecorMarket**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworlddecormarket) | **Get** /v1/world/decor/market | Decor copies other people are reselling (asks in COSM); optional pair filter. Read only: listing and buying stay on crossly.net.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldDecorMine**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworlddecormine) | **Get** /v1/world/decor/mine | Decor you hold AND decor you have minted to your wallet, newest first: ownershipId, cosmeticId, sku, name, surface, slot, tier, acquiredVia, bound, editionNumber of maxSupply, mintState (held | minting | minted), mintAddress, mintable, and what it unlocks. meta.available, meta.mint.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldDecorStalls**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworlddecorstalls) | **Get** /v1/world/decor/stalls | Your stall bookings, to dress their booths.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldDecorStreets**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworlddecorstreets) | **Get** /v1/world/decor/streets | Streets you may dress.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldDms**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworlddms) | **Get** /v1/world/dms | Your friends (mutual follows) for direct messages: each with userId, name, the last line between you (text, at, mine) or null, and how many of theirs you have not read; most recent first.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldFollowingRooms**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldfollowingrooms) | **Get** /v1/world/following/rooms | The public rooms of people you follow, to walk into.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldFollows**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldfollows) | **Get** /v1/world/follows | Everybody you follow in Crossly World, newest first, each with mutual: true when they follow you back (a friend).
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldFriendHere**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldfriendhere) | **Get** /v1/world/friends/here | Your friends in Crossly World right now, and where (when they share it).
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldFriends**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldfriends) | **Get** /v1/world/friends | Your friends (mutual follows), each with the PUBLIC room they are in right now when they chose to be seen by name there (else null), and their home address slug when they let friends visit. Invisible and anonymous friends are never located.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldGamePackages**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldgamepackages) | **Get** /v1/world/games/packages | Your game packages, each with its latest version and review state.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldGameRewards**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldgamerewards) | **Get** /v1/world/games/rewards | Rewards you won in games: granted, or recorded as unfunded for COSM and CBX (a game prints no value).
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldGames**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldgames) | **Get** /v1/world/games | The Game Directory: every live, reviewed game package with its players, places, rewards, contests and the piece people place.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldHomeBuilding**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldhomebuilding) | **Get** /v1/world/home/building | Homes whose owner made you a co-builder.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldInteractiveCatalogue**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldinteractivecatalogue) | **Get** /v1/world/interactive/catalogue | Interactive objects: every piece that runs a shared script in a room (first-party and approved creator designs). Per item: model (the piece key a home layout or boutique prop stores), decorValue (the home_prop value its SKU claims), source (first_party | creator), name, blurb, size [x, z] metres, fallbackMesh, cosmeticId (the SKU on the Decor Shop, null when not on sale) and version (a creator design: the approved version every copy runs). Read-only: buying is on crossly.net/shop/decor.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldInteractiveDesigns**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldinteractivedesigns) | **Get** /v1/world/interactive/designs | Your interactive-object designs (MML documents you wrote), newest first: id, handle, name, footprint (s | m | l | f), tint, priceBaseUnits (COSM, base-unit string), maxSupply, model, decorValue, cosmeticId, liveVersion (the approved version every copy runs; null &#x3D; none), withdrawnReason, archivedAt (you archived it: off sale, owned copies keep running; null &#x3D; not archived), latestVersion { version, state: pending | approved | rejected | withdrawn | cancelled, decisionNote }, inReview. &#x60;archived&#x60;: exclude (default), include or only archived designs. Read-only: writing, previewing, submitting and archiving happen on crossly.net/shop/decor?tab&#x3D;interactive.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldJobPosted**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldjobposted) | **Get** /v1/world/jobs/posted | Shifts you posted for your rooms, with who claimed each and how it went.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldOutfits**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldoutfits) | **Get** /v1/world/outfits | Your saved outfits (up to 6, oldest first): id, name, appearance (skin, hair, hairColour, eyes, build) and items -- each worn thing as the door it goes back on by (slot, door: cosmetic | receipt | stock, id, label). Put one on with POST /v1/world/outfits/:id/apply.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldPlaceBans**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldplacebans) | **Get** /v1/world/places/{slug}/bans | Who is banned from a place you own (your boutique or home).
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldPlaceBundles**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldplacebundles) | **Get** /v1/world/places/{slug}/bundles | The owner&#39;s bundle deals mapped onto this room&#39;s items (itemIds null &#x3D; any N from the shop). The discount is decided at checkout.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldPlaceCollections**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldplacecollections) | **Get** /v1/world/places/{slug}/collections | The owner&#39;s collection rooms: one per category, brand and model they collect.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldPlaceOffers**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldplaceoffers) | **Get** /v1/world/places/{slug}/offers | What is for sale in a public room: price, condition and stock, with market references. 404 when no such room is shared.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldPlaceRadar**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldplaceradar) | **Get** /v1/world/places/{slug}/radar | What you have that someone here wants, and what is here that you want: matched on catalog ids.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldPlaceTradeTables**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldplacetradetables) | **Get** /v1/world/places/{slug}/trade-tables | The open trade tables in a place: who is sitting and what is laid on the mat.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldPlaceTrades**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldplacetrades) | **Get** /v1/world/places/{slug}/trades | Items in this room open to trades, each with its trade post path.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldProvenance**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldprovenance) | **Get** /v1/world/provenance | Provenance trails: where in Crossly World your purchases were found, newest first (optionally ?listingIds&#x3D;a,b -- platform listing ids). data: [{ platformListingId, slug (the listing public slug), kind (room | window | stall | space | drop | auction | worn | agent), place (its name, or null when gone), at, line -- e.g. \&quot;Found at The Sneaker Vault, Oct 2026\&quot; }]. Yours only.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldQuests**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldquests) | **Get** /v1/world/quests | Every world quest with your progress: key, the pin it earns, goal, target, count, done, colour. meta.wearing is the pin on your nameplate (or null); meta.streak your current and best daily visit streak. Progress only comes from what the server saw -- there is no write of it.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldSpaceMine**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldspacemine) | **Get** /v1/world/spaces/mine | Spaces you belong to.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldSpaceRequests**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldspacerequests) | **Get** /v1/world/spaces/{slug}/requests | As host or co-host, who asked to join.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldSpaceWhatsOn**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldspacewhatson) | **Get** /v1/world/spaces/whats-on | Live and upcoming events across Spaces (streets&#x3D;1 and stage&#x3D;1 add street and Main Stage events).
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldSpaces**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldspaces) | **Get** /v1/world/spaces | The Spaces directory: seller-hosted clubs with a venue, members and events (optional venue filter).
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldStreaks**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldstreaks) | **Get** /v1/world/streaks | The longest current daily visit streaks (2+ days), top ten: name and days. Only people still choosing to be seen by name.
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldTradeMine**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldtrademine) | **Get** /v1/world/trades/mine | Your trades and each item progress through the authentication gate (verified or failed).
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldTradeTableBinder**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldtradetablebinder) | **Get** /v1/world/trade-tables/binder | Your binder: unsold collection rows that are catalog items and may be laid on a trade mat -- key, variantId, condition, grade, portfolioId, name, image and valueCents (market value, cents).
*WorldApi* | [**ListWorldTradeTableListings**](docs/WorldApi.md#listworldtradetablelistings) | **Get** /v1/world/trade-tables/listings | Your open Trade Block trade listings a trade table can be opened on in the world, each with its items&#39; names.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateSpatialSceneCosmetic**](docs/WorldApi.md#updatespatialscenecosmetic) | **Patch** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/cosmetics | Redecorate one of your rooms (wall, floor, lighting, trim...). Unknown keys are dropped; the response is what survived. &#x60;clear&#x60; lists keys to reset.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateSpatialSceneNode**](docs/WorldApi.md#updatespatialscenenode) | **Patch** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/nodes/{nodeId} | Move a rack in one of your warehouse rooms (position in metres).
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateSpatialSceneSetting**](docs/WorldApi.md#updatespatialscenesetting) | **Patch** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/settings | How one of your rooms is laid out and lit: grouping and sort keys, theme, overlay and the arrival camera. Cosmetics and the shop layout have their own writers.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateSpatialSceneShopLayout**](docs/WorldApi.md#updatespatialsceneshoplayout) | **Put** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/shop/layout | Save your shop room&#39;s sections and displays (validated server-side; the response is the stored layout).
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateSpatialSceneWindow**](docs/WorldApi.md#updatespatialscenewindow) | **Put** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/window | Choose up to three of your live Crossly listings for the street window of your shop, in slot order. Picks that sell drop out and the newest fill in; an empty list goes back to newest first.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateSpatialStreetDecor**](docs/WorldApi.md#updatespatialstreetdecor) | **Put** /v1/spatial/streets/{slug}/decor | Dress a street you own with decor you hold (validated against your inventory).
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateSpatialStreetOwned**](docs/WorldApi.md#updatespatialstreetowned) | **Patch** /v1/spatial/streets/owned/{id} | Who may open a shop on a street you own (open, apply or invite).
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldAutoShop**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldautoshop) | **Patch** /v1/world/auto-shop | Turn your automatic world shop off ({ enabled: false } -- an auto-created shop is made private, never deleted) or back on. Answers the new status.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldAvatarIdentity**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldavataridentity) | **Put** /v1/world/avatar/identity | Choose named or anonymous. Rooms you are already in keep the old answer until you rejoin.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldAvatarLook**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldavatarlook) | **Put** /v1/world/avatar/look | Save your world look to the account, so every device starts from it. look: null stays anonymous.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldAvatarNftBadge**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldavatarnftbadge) | **Put** /v1/world/avatar/nft-badge | Wear an NFT you hold on your nameplate (null to take it off). Checked again each time you join a room.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldCapabilityWear**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldcapabilitywear) | **Put** /v1/world/capabilities/wear | Wear a held token on an avatar slot (refId from GET /v1/world/capabilities), or pass refId null to take it off. Its collection must declare a wear capability for that slot.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldCollectionCapability**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldcollectioncapability) | **Put** /v1/world/collections/{chain}/{contract}/capabilities | Declare what the tokens of a collection can do (the whole declaration, spec crossly.nft-capabilities/1). As its creator (launched on Crossly or claimed) any kind; as a holder, cosmetic kinds only. Replaces your previous declaration; pending if it names an uploaded asset or an always-reviewed kind.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldConventionStatus**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldconventionstatus) | **Put** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/status | Open, start, end or cancel a convention you host.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldCosmDesignChainAutoUnlock**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldcosmdesignchainautounlock) | **Put** /v1/world/cosm/designs/{id}/chain-auto-unlock | Opt one of your COSM designs in or out of the free chain unlock it earns when popular (on by default). Answers its popularity.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldEmoteWheel**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldemotewheel) | **Put** /v1/world/emotes/wheel | Arrange your emote wheel: up to 8 slots, each an emote id, a moment slot or null. Returns the saved wheel (locked or unknown ids are dropped).
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldFitValueShare**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldfitvalueshare) | **Put** /v1/world/fit-value/share | Whether people in a room can see what your outfit is worth.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldHome**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldhome) | **Patch** /v1/world/home | Your home: privacy (open, friends, private), theme, and wall, floor and mood decor you hold.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldHomeBuilder**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldhomebuilder) | **Put** /v1/world/home/builders | Set the co-builders of your home: { userIds } -- friends (mutual follows) only, at most 4, replacing the list ([] clears it). They may rearrange your home in the world with your things and add free furniture, never your bought pieces. New co-builders get a world notice.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldHomeLayout**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldhomelayout) | **Put** /v1/world/home/layout | Arrange your home: the full layout (up to 200 pieces you own), validated server-side.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldHomeLayoutBySlug**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldhomelayoutbyslug) | **Put** /v1/world/homes/{slug}/layout | Rearrange a home you co-build (never adding the owner bought pieces).
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldInteractiveDesignDraft**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldinteractivedesigndraft) | **Put** /v1/world/interactive/designs/{id}/draft | Save the draft MML source (up to 64 KB); the response carries its static check.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldListingHoldersOnly**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldlistingholdersonly) | **Put** /v1/world/listings/{id}/holders-only | Reserve one of your listings for holders of a collection you declared a holders_only perk for (or release it). Checkout refuses anybody else.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldOutfit**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldoutfit) | **Patch** /v1/world/outfits/{id} | Rename a saved look: { name (1-32 chars) }. 404 when it is not yours.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldPetActive**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldpetactive) | **Put** /v1/world/pets/active | Walk one of your pets with you in the world, or none (kind: null).
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldQuestPin**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldquestpin) | **Put** /v1/world/quests/pin | Wear a pin you EARNED from a quest on your nameplate, or { pin: null } to wear none. 403 for a pin you have not earned. Shows from the next room you walk into.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldRoomHolderGate**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldroomholdergate) | **Put** /v1/world/rooms/{id}/holder-gate | Make your room holders-only (its collection rooms follow). Send null to open it again. Returns &#x60;{ gate }&#x60;.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldSpace**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldspace) | **Patch** /v1/world/spaces/{slug} | As host, change a Space name, about or membership.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldSpaceHolderGate**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldspaceholdergate) | **Put** /v1/world/spaces/{slug}/holder-gate | Make your Space holders-only: only holders of an NFT collection join it (others can look in). Send null to open it again. Returns &#x60;{ gate }&#x60;.
*WorldApi* | [**UpdateWorldStallBookingBooth**](docs/WorldApi.md#updateworldstallbookingbooth) | **Put** /v1/world/stalls/bookings/{id}/booth | Dress the booth of a stall you booked (decor you hold).


## Where things are

| what | where |
|---|---|
| one class per API group (`OrdersApi`, `ListingsApi`, …) | `api_*.go` (package root) |
| one model per response/request type, grouped by product area | `model_<area>.go` (package root) |
| a reference page per API group, every method with an example | `docs/` |

The area is the word after the verb in the model name: `GetVaultLendResponse`
is under `vault`, `ListOrdersItem` under `order`. Areas with only a handful of
models, and small shared shapes, are under `common`.

Models are named after where they appear: `GetOrderResponse` is what `getOrder`
returns, `GetOrderResponseDataLines` the type of its `data.lines`, `ListOrdersItem`
one row of `listOrders`. A type several endpoints share appears ONCE, under the
first name it was given — or, for small shared shapes like `{ ok }`, a name from
its fields (`OkResponse`). There is no per-model doc page: the model source file
is the reference, and your editor will jump to it.

## Documentation For Authorization



### PersonalAccessToken

- **Type**: HTTP Bearer token authentication

Example

```golang
auth := context.WithValue(context.Background(), sw.ContextAccessToken, "BEARER_TOKEN_STRING")
r, err := client.Service.Operation(auth, args)
```


## Documentation for Utility Methods

Due to the fact that model structure members are all pointers, this package contains
a number of utility functions to easily obtain pointers to values of basic types.
Each of these functions takes a value of the given basic type and returns a pointer to it:

* `PtrBool`
* `PtrInt`
* `PtrInt32`
* `PtrInt64`
* `PtrFloat`
* `PtrFloat32`
* `PtrFloat64`
* `PtrString`
* `PtrTime`
